===== app/options/backup-restore/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import Box from "@/components/Box";
import Button from "@/components/Button";
import Description from "@/components/Description";
import NavbarTitle from "@/components/NavbarTitle";
import { AccountType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";
import { BackupType, getBackup } from "@/utility/backup-restore/methods";
import { getAll } from "@/utility/db/Database";
import { DatabaseStores } from "@/utility/keys/database";
import { TagType } from "@/utility/shared/tagTypes";
import { TransactionType } from "@/utility/transactions/types";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { ChangeEvent, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { RESTORE_DATA_KEY } from "./restore/page";

export default function Page() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const [fileName, setFileName] = useState<string>("");
  const [backupData, setBackupData] = useState<BackupType>();

  const [accounts, setAccounts] = useState<AccountType[]>([]);
  const [transactions, setTransactions] = useState<TransactionType[]>([]);
  const [tags, setTags] = useState<TagType[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const { accountsStore, transactionsStore, tagsStore } = DatabaseStores;

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try {
        setAccounts(await getAll<AccountType>(accountsStore));
        setTransactions(await getAll<TransactionType>(transactionsStore));
        setTags(await getAll<TagType>(tagsStore));
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error("Backup load error:", err);
        setError(err.message || "خطا در خواندن اطلاعات از دیتابیس");
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    })();
  }, [accountsStore, transactionsStore, tagsStore]);

  function handleFileChange(event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const file = event.target.files?.[0];

    if (file) {
      const fileReader = new FileReader();
      fileReader.onload = (e) => {
        try {
          const result: BackupType = JSON.parse(fileReader.result as string);
          const backupTime = new Date(result.time).toLocaleDateString("fa-ir");

          setFileName("تاریخ نسخه پشتیبان: " + backupTime);
          setBackupData(result);
        } catch {
          setError("فایل پشتیبان انتخاب شده معتبر نیست.");
        }
      };
      fileReader.readAsText(file);
    }
  }

  function handleFilePicker() {
    fileInputRef.current?.click();
  }

  const handleBackupRestore = () => {
    if (!backupData) return;

    sessionStorage.setItem(RESTORE_DATA_KEY, JSON.stringify(backupData));
    router.push("/options/backup-restore/restore");
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-7.5 grid gap-15 animate-fade-in">
      <div className="grid gap-7.5">
        <NavbarTitle title="پشتیبان‌گیری و بازیابی" />
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div className="bg-red-background border border-red-border text-red text-xs p-4 rounded-2xl">
          {error}
          <br />
          <span className="text-secondary">
            (لطفاً مطمئن شوید دستورات تغییر نوع ستون‌ها را در دیتابیس اجرا
            کرده‌اید)
          </span>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="grid gap-10">
        <Box className="gap-5">
          <h1 className="text-sm">پشتیبان‌گیری از اطلاعات</h1>

          <Description>
            باتوجه به این‌که اطلاعات در دستگاه به صورت محلی ذخیره میشه و در هیچ
            سرویس ابری‌ای پشتیبان گرفته نمیشه، توصیه میشه به صورت دوره‌ای نسخه
            پشتیبان تهیه کنین.
          </Description>

          <Description>
            اطلاعاتی که در نسخه پشتیبان ذخیره میشه شامل{" "}
            <b className="text-primary">حساب‌ها</b>،{" "}
            <b className="text-primary">برچسب‌ها</b> و{" "}
            <b className="text-primary">تراکنش‌ها</b> هست.
          </Description>

          <Button
            disabled={isLoading}
            onClick={() => getBackup(accounts, transactions, tags)}
          >
            {isLoading ? "در حال آماده‌سازی..." : "بارگیری نسخه پشتیبان"}
          </Button>
        </Box>

        <Box className="gap-5">
          <h1 className="text-sm">بازیابی اطلاعات</h1>
          <Description>
            اگه یه نسخه پشتیبان دارید، میتونید اون‌رو بارگذاری کنید تا
            اطلاعات‌تون بازیابی بشه؛ درنظر داشته باشید که اگه تا الان اطلاعاتی
            رو در برنامه وارد کردین، این اطلاعات در ابتدا{" "}
            <b className="text-red">حذف</b> و سپس اطلاعات موجود در نسخه پشتیبان
            وارد برنامه میشه.
          </Description>

          <input
            type="file"
            ref={fileInputRef}
            onChange={handleFileChange}
            accept=".pbak"
            className="hidden"
            hidden
          />
          <Button secondary onClick={handleFilePicker}>
            {!!fileName ? fileName : "بارگذاری نسخه پشتیبان"}
          </Button>
          <Button
            disabled={!backupData || isLoading}
            onClick={handleBackupRestore}
          >
            بازیابی اطلاعات
          </Button>
        </Box>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

و


===== app/options/delete-all/delete/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import Button from "@/components/Button";
import Info from "@/icons/Info";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { balancesStore } from "@/utility/balance/service/balancesStore";
import { AccountType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";
import { TransactionType } from "@/utility/transactions/types";
import { getAll } from "@/utility/db/Database";
import { DatabaseStores } from "@/utility/keys/database";
import { SplashShownKey } from "@/utility/keys/sessionStore";
import ProcessingOverlay from "@/components/ProcessingOverlay";

export default function DeleteAllConfirm() {
  const [accounts, setAccounts] = useState<AccountType[]>([]);
  const [transactions, setTransactions] = useState<TransactionType[]>([]);
  const router = useRouter();
  const [validate, setValidate] = useState(false);
  const [isProcessing, setIsProcessing] = useState(false);
  const { accountsStore, transactionsStore } = DatabaseStores;

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      setAccounts(await getAll<AccountType>(accountsStore));
      setTransactions(await getAll<TransactionType>(transactionsStore));
    })();
  }, []);

  const handleDeleteAll = async () => {
    if (!validate) return;
    setIsProcessing(true);

    try {
      await fetch("/api/all", { method: "DELETE" });
      balancesStore.clear();
      sessionStorage.removeItem(SplashShownKey);
      router.replace("/splash");
    } catch (error) {
      setIsProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  if (isProcessing) {
    return (
      <ProcessingOverlay
        title="در حال پاکسازی اطلاعات"
        subtitle="در حال بازنشایی برنامه..."
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-7.5 p-15 h-screen">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
        <Info className="fill-red" height={30} width={30} />
        <h1 className="text-xl">هشدار</h1>
      </div>

      <p className="text-justify [text-align-last:center] text-xs w-6/7">
        با این‌کار تمام اطلاعات{" "}
        <span className="text-red">
          {accounts.length.toLocaleString("fa-IR")} حساب
        </span>{" "}
        شامل{" "}
        <span className="text-red">
          {transactions.length.toLocaleString("fa-IR")} تراکنش
        </span>{" "}
        حذف میشه؛ مطمئن هستین؟
      </p>

      <div className="grid gap-4">
        <label className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer text-xs">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            onChange={(e) => setValidate(e.target.checked)}
            className="accent-red p-3 ml-1"
          />
          بله، مطمئن هستم
        </label>
      </div>

      <Button
        red={validate}
        secondary={!validate}
        disabled={!validate}
        onClick={handleDeleteAll}
      >
        حذف همه اطلاعات
      </Button>
      <Button secondary onClick={() => router.back()}>
        لغو
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

            حذف همه‌ی اطلاعات
          </Button>

===== app/options/import/components/ShowDataPreview.tsx =====

import Box from "@/components/Box";
import Button from "@/components/Button";
import Title from "@/components/SectionTitle";
import { TagItemTypes } from "@/utility/shared/tagTypes";
import { AccountTypes } from "@/utility/shared/accountsTypes";
import {
  excelColorOptions,
  PreviewData,
} from "@/utility/import/excelConstants";

interface PreviewSectionProps {
  previewData: PreviewData;
  isConfirmed: boolean;
  setIsConfirmed: (value: boolean) => void;
  isImporting: boolean;
  handleImport: () => void;
}

export default function PreviewSection({
  previewData,
  isConfirmed,
  setIsConfirmed,
  isImporting,
  handleImport,
}: PreviewSectionProps) {
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-10 w-full max-w-full min-w-0">
      <Title>بازبینی اطلاعات</Title>

            تعداد حساب‌های یافت شده:{" "}

            تعداد برچسب‌های یافت شده:{" "}
            {previewData.tags.length.toLocaleString("fa-IR")}
          </div>
        </div>
      </Box>

      <Box className="min-w-0">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold mb-4">حساب‌ها</h3>
        <div className="overflow-x-auto w-full">
          <table className="w-full text-xs text-right whitespace-nowrap min-w-full">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th className="bg-primary text-white p-3">ردیف</th>
                <th className="bg-primary text-white p-3">نام حساب</th>
                <th className="bg-primary text-white p-3">نوع</th>
                <th className="bg-primary text-white p-3">رنگ</th>
                <th className="bg-primary text-white p-3">وضعیت</th>
                <th className="bg-primary text-white p-3">تعداد تراکنش‌ها</th>
                <th className="bg-primary text-white p-3">موجودی نهایی</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {previewData.accounts.map((acc, i) => (
                <tr key={i} className="border-t border-elements-border">
                  <td className="p-3">{acc.order}</td>
                  <td className="p-3">{acc.name}</td>
                  <td className="p-3">
                    {acc.default
                      ? "پیش‌فرض"
                      : (AccountTypes as any)[acc.type]?.name}
                  </td>
                  <td className="p-3">
                    {acc.default
                      ? "پیش‌فرض"
                      : excelColorOptions.find((c) => c.key === acc.color)
                          ?.label}
                  </td>
                  <td className="p-3">
                    {acc.isNew ? (
                      <span className="text-green-600 font-bold">جدید</span>
                    ) : acc.needsUpdate ? (
                      <span className="text-blue-600 font-bold">بروزرسانی</span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-yellow-600 font-bold">موجود</span>
                    )}
                  </td>
                  <td className="p-3">{acc.txCount.toLocaleString("fa-IR")}</td>
                  <td className="p-3">
                    {acc.finalBalance.toLocaleString("fa-IR")} ﷼
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </Box>

      <Box className="min-w-0">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold mb-4">برچسب‌ها</h3>
        <div className="overflow-x-auto w-full">
          <table className="w-full text-xs text-right whitespace-nowrap min-w-full">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th className="bg-primary text-white p-3">ردیف</th>
                <th className="bg-primary text-white p-3">نام برچسب</th>
                <th className="bg-primary text-white p-3">نوع</th>
                <th className="bg-primary text-white p-3">وضعیت</th>
                <th className="bg-primary text-white p-3">تعداد استفاده</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {previewData.tags.map((tag, i) => (
                <tr key={i} className="border-t border-elements-border">
                  <td className="p-3">{tag.order}</td>
                  <td className="p-3">{tag.name}</td>
                  <td className="p-3">
                    {TagItemTypes.find((t) => t.value === tag.type)?.label}
                  </td>
                  <td className="p-3">
                    {tag.isNew ? (
                      <span className="text-green-600 font-bold">جدید</span>
                    ) : tag.needsUpdate ? (
                      <span className="text-blue-600 font-bold">بروزرسانی</span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-yellow-600 font-bold">موجود</span>
                    )}
                  </td>
                  <td className="p-3">{tag.txCount.toLocaleString("fa-IR")}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </Box>

      <Box>
        <div className="grid gap-4">
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs cursor-pointer">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={isConfirmed}
              onChange={(e) => setIsConfirmed(e.target.checked)}
              className="accent-primary p-3"
            />
            تایید می‌کنم که اطلاعات بررسی شد و می‌خواهم به دیتابیس فعلی اضافه
            شوند.
          </label>
          <Button
            secondary={!isConfirmed}
            disabled={!isConfirmed || isImporting}
            onClick={handleImport}
          >
            {isImporting ? "در حال وارد کردن..." : "ورود اطلاعات به برنامه"}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </Box>
    </div>
  );
}

===== app/options/import/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import Box from "@/components/Box";
import Button from "@/components/Button";
import NavbarTitle from "@/components/NavbarTitle";
import { DatabaseStores } from "@/utility/keys/database";
import { getAll } from "@/utility/db/Database";
import { AccountType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";
import { TagType } from "@/utility/shared/tagTypes";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import Description from "@/components/Description";
import { generateExcelTemplate } from "@/utility/import/generateTemplate";
import { importDataToDatabase } from "@/utility/import/importToDatabase";
import { parseUploadedExcel } from "@/utility/import/parseExcelFile";
import PreviewSection from "./components/ShowDataPreview";
import { PreviewData } from "@/utility/import/excelConstants";
import ProcessingOverlay from "@/components/ProcessingOverlay";

export default function ImportExcelPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const [existingAccounts, setExistingAccounts] = useState<AccountType[]>([]);
  const [existingTags, setExistingTags] = useState<TagType[]>([]);

  const [previewData, setPreviewData] = useState<PreviewData | null>(null);
  const [isConfirmed, setIsConfirmed] = useState(false);
  const [isImporting, setIsImporting] = useState(false); // ✅ استیت لودینگ
  const [fileError, setFileError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadInitial() {
      const accs = await getAll<AccountType>(DatabaseStores.accountsStore);
      accs.sort((a, b) => (a.order ?? 9999) - (b.order ?? 9999));
      setExistingAccounts(accs);

      const tags = await getAll<TagType>(DatabaseStores.tagsStore);
      tags.sort((a, b) => (a.order ?? 9999) - (b.order ?? 9999));
      setExistingTags(tags);
    }
    loadInitial();
  }, []);

  const handleDownloadTemplate = async () => {
    await generateExcelTemplate(existingAccounts, existingTags);
  };

  const handleFileUpload = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    setFileError(null);
    setPreviewData(null);

    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = async (event) => {
      const buffer = event.target?.result as ArrayBuffer;
      try {
        const parsedData = await parseUploadedExcel(
          buffer,
          existingAccounts,
          existingTags,
        );
        setPreviewData(parsedData);
      } catch (err: any) {
        setFileError(err.message || "خطایی در پردازش فایل رخ داد.");
      }
    };
    reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
  };

  const handleImport = async () => {
    if (!isConfirmed || !previewData) return;
    setIsImporting(true);

    try {
      await importDataToDatabase(
        previewData,
        existingAccounts,
        existingTags,
        router,
      );
    } catch (error) {
      setIsImporting(false);
      setFileError("خطایی در هنگام ورود اطلاعات رخ داد.");
    }
  };

  if (isImporting) {
    return (
      <ProcessingOverlay
        title="در حال وارد کردن اطلاعات"
        subtitle="لطفاً تا پایان عملیات صبر کنید..."
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-7.5 grid gap-15 w-full max-w-full overflow-hidden">
      <NavbarTitle title="ورود اطلاعات از اکسل" />

      <div className="grid gap-5">
        <Box>
          <h3 className="text-sm font-bold">مرحله ۱: دانلود فایل نمونه</h3>
          <Description>
            فایل اکسل نمونه را دانلود کنید. در صفحه اول، حساب‌ها و برچسب‌های
            فعلی شما همراه با کد آن‌ها وجود دارد. موارد جدید را بدون کد در
            انتهای لیست اضافه کنید.
          </Description>
          <Button onClick={handleDownloadTemplate}>دانلود فایل نمونه</Button>
        </Box>

        <Box>
          <h3 className="text-sm font-bold">مرحله ۲: آپلود فایل تکمیل شده</h3>
          <input
            type="file"
            accept=".xlsx, .xls"
            ref={fileInputRef}
            onChange={handleFileUpload}
            className="hidden"
          />
          <Button secondary onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}>
            انتخاب فایل اکسل
          </Button>
          {fileError && (
            <div className="mt-4 text-red-500 font-bold text-sm">
              {fileError}
            </div>
          )}
        </Box>
      </div>

      {previewData && (
        <PreviewSection
          previewData={previewData}
          isConfirmed={isConfirmed}
          setIsConfirmed={setIsConfirmed}
          isImporting={isImporting}
          handleImport={handleImport}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import ListElement from "@/components/ListElement";

===== app/options/tags/add/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import Capsule from "@/components/Capsule";
import NavbarTitle from "@/components/NavbarTitle";
import Left from "@/icons/Left";
import { TagType } from "@/utility/shared/tagTypes";
import { ChangeEvent, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { getAll, putItem } from "@/utility/db/Database";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { TagItemTypes } from "@/utility/shared/tagTypes";

export default function AddTag() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [tagDetails, setTagDetails] = useState<TagType>({
    id: crypto.randomUUID(),
    name: "",
    type: "Person",
    order: 0,
  });
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const id = searchParams.get("id");

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      const tags = await getAll<TagType>("tags");

      if (!!id) {
        const tagInDB = tags.find((item) => item.id === id);
        setTagDetails(tagInDB!);
      } else {
        const nextId = crypto.randomUUID();
        setTagDetails((prev) => ({ ...prev, id: nextId, order: tags.length }));
      }
    })();
  }, []);

  const isValid = useMemo(() => {
    return tagDetails.name.trim().length >= 3;
  }, [tagDetails.name]);

  function handleNameChange(e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void {
    setTagDetails((prevTag) => ({ ...prevTag, name: e.target.value }));
  }

  async function handleSave() {
    if (isValid) {
      await putItem("tags", tagDetails);
      router.back();
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-7.5 flex flex-col gap-15">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-7.5">
        <NavbarTitle title="برچسب جدید">
          <Capsule Icon={Left} primary={isValid} onClick={handleSave}>
            ذخیره برچسب
          </Capsule>
        </NavbarTitle>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-7.5">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs">نام برچسب</span>
          <input
            type="text"
            onChange={handleNameChange}
            placeholder="شرکت فلان"
            className="h-12 text-sm border border-elements-border bg-elements-background px-4 rounded-2xl outline-none focus:bg-primary-background focus:border-primary-border transition-all"
            value={tagDetails.name}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs">نوع برچسب</span>
          <div className="bg-elements-border/75 grid grid-cols-3 justify-between w-fit mx-auto gap-2 p-2 rounded-2xl">
            {TagItemTypes.map((item) => (
              <div
                key={item.value}
                className={`text-xs text-center p-2.5 rounded-xl transition-all text-nowrap ${item.value === tagDetails.type ? "bg-primary text-white" : "bg-white"}`}
                onClick={() =>
                  setTagDetails((prevTag) => ({ ...prevTag, type: item.value }))
                }
              >
                {item.label}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

و


===== app/options/tags/delete/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import Button from "@/components/Button";
import Info from "@/icons/Info";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { deleteItem, getAll, getItem, putItem } from "@/utility/db/Database";
import { TransactionType } from "@/utility/transactions/types";
import { DatabaseStores } from "@/utility/keys/database";
import { TagType } from "@/utility/shared/tagTypes";

export default function TagDelete() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const tagID = searchParams.get("id") ?? "";
  const router = useRouter();
  const { tagsStore, transactionsStore } = DatabaseStores;

  const [tag, setTag] = useState<TagType>();
  const [transactionsCount, setTransactionsCount] = useState("۰");

  const [validate, setValidate] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      setTag((await getItem<TagType>(tagsStore, tagID)) ?? undefined);

      setTransactionsCount(
        (await getAll<TransactionType>(transactionsStore))
          .filter((item) => item.tag === tagID)
          .length.toLocaleString("fa-IR"),
      );
    })();
  }, [tagID, tagsStore, transactionsStore, router]);

  const handleDelete = async () => {
    if (!validate) return;

    const transactions = (await getAll(transactionsStore)) as TransactionType[];
    const toChange = transactions.filter((t) => t.tag === tagID);
    for (const tx of toChange) {
      await putItem(transactionsStore, { ...tx, tag: undefined });
    }

    await deleteItem(tagsStore, tagID);

    router.back();
  };

  if (!tag) return null;

  return (
    <div className="grid place-content-center gap-7.5 p-15 h-screen">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
        <Info className="fill-red" height={30} width={30} />
        <h1 className="text-xl">هشدار</h1>
      </div>

      <p className="text-justify [text-align-last:center] text-xs">
        با این‌کار برچسب <span className="font-bold">{tag.name}</span> که در{" "}
        {transactionsCount} تراکنش استفاده شده{" "}
        <span className="text-red">حذف</span> میشه؛ مطمئن هستین؟ (برچسب{" "}
        <span className="font-bold">{tag.name}</span> از همه‌ی این تراکنش‌ها
        برداشته میشه)
      </p>

      <div className="grid gap-4">
        <label className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer text-xs">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={validate}
            onChange={(e) => setValidate(e.target.checked)}
            className="accent-red p-3 ml-1"
          />
          بله، مطمئن هستم
        </label>

        <Button
          red={validate}
          secondary={!validate}
          disabled={!validate}
          className="transition-all"
          onClick={handleDelete}
        >
          حذف حساب
        </Button>
      </div>

      <Button secondary onClick={() => router.back()}>
        لغو
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

===== app/options/tags/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import Capsule from "@/components/Capsule";
import Description from "@/components/Description";
import ListElement from "@/components/ListElement";
import NavbarTitle from "@/components/NavbarTitle";
import SwipeableElement from "@/components/SwipeableElement";
import Left from "@/icons/Left";
import Tag from "@/icons/Tag";
import { PrimaryColors } from "@/utility/shared/colors";
import { getAll } from "@/utility/db/Database";
import { TagItemTypes } from "@/utility/shared/tagTypes";
import { TagType } from "@/utility/shared/tagTypes";
import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function Tags() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [tags, setTags] = useState<TagType[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [openItemId, setOpenItemId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try {
        setTags(await getAll<TagType>("tags"));
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Failed to load tags:", error);
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    })();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (openItemId === null) return;
    const handleCloseAll = () => setOpenItemId(null);
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      window.addEventListener("mouseup", handleCloseAll);
      window.addEventListener("touchend", handleCloseAll);
    }, 0);
    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      window.removeEventListener("mouseup", handleCloseAll);
      window.removeEventListener("touchend", handleCloseAll);
    };
  }, [openItemId]);

  return (
    <div className="p-7.5 flex flex-col items-center gap-15 w-screen min-h-screen animate-fade-in">
      <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-7.5 w-full">
        <NavbarTitle title="برچسب‌ها">
          <Capsule Icon={Left} href={`${pathname}/add`}>
            برچسب جدید
          </Capsule>
        </NavbarTitle>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 min-h-100 w-full">
        {isLoading ? (
          <>
            {[1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
              <div
                key={i}
                className="w-full p-4 flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-2xl border border-elements-border bg-elements-background animate-pulse"
              >
                <div className="h-7.5 w-7.5 rounded-4xl bg-elements-border shrink-0"></div>
                <div className="grid gap-2 w-full">
                  <div className="h-4 w-24 rounded-md bg-elements-border"></div>
                  <div className="h-3 w-16 rounded-md bg-elements-border"></div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </>
        ) : tags.length ? (
          tags.map((item) => (
            <SwipeableElement
              key={item.id}
              isOpen={openItemId === item.id}
              onOpenChange={(open) => setOpenItemId(open ? item.id : null)}
              onCloseOthers={() => setOpenItemId(null)}
              onEdit={() => {
                router.push(`${pathname}/add?id=${item.id}`);
              }}
              onDelete={async () => {
                router.push(`${pathname}/delete?id=${item.id}`);
              }}
            >
              <ListElement
                href="#"
                iconColor={PrimaryColors.primary}
                Icon={Tag}
                children={item.name}
                desc={
                  TagItemTypes.find((tag) => tag.value === item.type)?.label
                }
              />
            </SwipeableElement>
          ))
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-10 h-full">
            <Tag className="fill-secondary" height={75} width={75} />
            <Description>تا الان هیچ برچسبی اضافه نکردین.</Description>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== app/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import AccountCard from "@/components/AccountCard";
import Button from "@/components/Button";
import ChartContainer from "@/components/ChartContainer";
import QuickAccess from "@/app/components/QuickAccess";
import Title from "@/components/SectionTitle";
import useBalances from "@/utility/balance/service/useBalances";
import { getLatestTransactions } from "@/utility/shared/transactionsMethods";
import { getAccountBalances } from "@/utility/shared/balancesMethods";
import { IconComponent } from "@/icons/types";
import Receive from "@/icons/Receive";
import Send from "@/icons/Send";
import Setting from "@/icons/Setting";
import ThreeDots from "@/icons/ThreeDots";
import Transfer from "@/icons/Transfer";
import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { deleteItem, getAll } from "@/utility/db/Database";
import { DatabaseStores } from "@/utility/keys/database";
import { AccountType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";
import { TransactionType } from "@/utility/transactions/types";
import { SplashShownKey } from "@/utility/keys/sessionStore";
import SwipeableElement from "@/components/SwipeableElement";
import TransactionItem from "@/components/TransactionItem";
import { usePageShowSync } from "@/utility/shared/usePageShowSync";
import AccountCardLoading from "@/components/AccountCardLoading";
import TransactionItemLoading from "@/components/TransactionItemLoading";
import ChartContainerLoading from "@/components/ChartContainerLoading";

export default function Home() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { accountsStore, transactionsStore } = DatabaseStores;
  const [accounts, setAccounts] = useState<AccountType[]>([]);
  const [transactions, setTransactions] = useState<TransactionType[]>([]);
  const { getBalances, setBalances } = useBalances();

  const [splashGuard, setSplashGuard] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [swipedItem, setSwipedItem] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [headerDate, setHeaderDate] = useState("");
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const pathname = usePathname();

  const accountsScrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!sessionStorage.getItem(SplashShownKey)) router.replace("/splash");
    else setSplashGuard(false);
  }, [router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  }, [pathname]);

  const loadData = async () => {
    const isFirstLoad = accounts.length === 0 && transactions.length === 0;
    if (isFirstLoad) setIsLoading(true);

    const [accs, txs] = await Promise.all([
      getAll<AccountType>(accountsStore),
      getAll<TransactionType>(transactionsStore),
    ]);

    setAccounts(accs.sort((a, b) => (a.order ?? 0) - (b.order ?? 0)));
    setTransactions(txs);
    setBalances(getAccountBalances(accs, txs));

    if (isFirstLoad) setIsLoading(false);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, [accountsStore, transactionsStore]);

  const resetAccountsScroll = () => {
    if (accountsScrollRef.current) {
      accountsScrollRef.current.scrollTo({ left: 0, behavior: "auto" });
    }
  };

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    resetAccountsScroll();
    const raf = requestAnimationFrame(resetAccountsScroll);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
  }, [isLoading, accounts]);

  usePageShowSync(() => {
    loadData();
    requestAnimationFrame(resetAccountsScroll);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    setHeaderDate(
      new Date().toLocaleDateString("fa-IR", {
        weekday: "long",
        month: "long",
        day: "numeric",
      }),
    );
  }, [pathname]);

  const latestTransactions = useMemo(() => {
    return getLatestTransactions(transactions, 5);
  }, [transactions]);

  const quickAccessList: { name: string; link: string; icon: IconComponent }[] =
    [
      { name: "دریافت", link: "/transactions/add?type=debit", icon: Receive },
      { name: "پرداخت", link: "/transactions/add?type=credit", icon: Send },
      {
        name: "انتقال",
        link: "/transactions/add?type=transfer",
        icon: Transfer,
      },
    ];

  if (splashGuard) return null;

  const handleDeleteTransaction = async (id: string) => {
    await deleteItem(transactionsStore, id);
    const newTxs = transactions.filter((t) => t.id !== id);
    setTransactions(newTxs);
    setBalances(getAccountBalances(accounts, newTxs));
    setSwipedItem(null);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-15 p-7.5 pb-15 overflow-x-hidden">
      <header className="flex justify-between p-2.5">
        <div className="flex w-7.5"></div>
        <h3 className="text-secondary text-center">{headerDate}</h3>
        <Link href="/options">
          <ThreeDots className="fill-primary" height={30} width={30} />
        </Link>
      </header>

      <div className="grid gap-7.5">
        <Link href="/accounts" className="w-fit">
          <Title>
            <div className="flex gap-0.5 items-center">
              <div>حساب‌ها</div>
              <Setting height={25} className="fill-primary" />
            </div>
          </Title>
        </Link>

        <div
          ref={accountsScrollRef}
          className={`flex gap-10 overflow-x-auto snap-x snap-mandatory px-[calc(50vw)] scrollbar-none -m-7.5 mb-0 py-7.5 ${!isLoading ? "animate-fade-in" : ""}`}
        >
          {isLoading ? (
            <AccountCardLoading />
          ) : (
            accounts.map(
              (account) =>
                !account.hidden && (
                  <AccountCard
                    key={account.id}
                    lastBalance={
                      getBalances()
                        .find((item) => item.id === account.id)
                        ?.history?.at(-1)?.balance ?? 0
                    }
                    {...account}
                  />
                ),
            )
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-16 w-full content-around justify-center">
        {quickAccessList.map((item) => (
          <QuickAccess key={item.link} Icon={item.icon} link={item.link}>
            {item.name}
          </QuickAccess>
        ))}
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <ChartContainerLoading />
      ) : (
        <ChartContainer
          title={"روند موجودی"}
          transactions={transactions}
          timeSelector
          link="/analyze"
          linkTitle="تحلیل و بررسی پیشرفته"
        />
      )}

      <div className={`grid gap-7.5 ${!isLoading ? "animate-fade-in" : ""}`}>
        <Title>تراکنش‌های اخیر</Title>

        <div className="flex flex-col w-full gap-5">
          {isLoading ? (
            <>
              <TransactionItemLoading />
              <TransactionItemLoading />
              <TransactionItemLoading />
            </>
          ) : (
            latestTransactions.map((transaction) => {
              const accountID =
                transaction.type === "credit"
                  ? transaction.credit
                  : transaction.debit;
              const account = accounts.find((acc) => acc.id === accountID);
              if (!account) return null;

              return (
                <SwipeableElement
                  isOpen={transaction.id === swipedItem}
                  onOpenChange={() => setSwipedItem(transaction.id)}
                  onCloseOthers={() => setSwipedItem(null)}
                  onEdit={() =>
                    transaction.type !== "initial"
                      ? router.push(`/transactions/edit?id=${transaction.id}`)
                      : router.push(`/accounts/edit?id=${account.id}`)
                  }
                  onDelete={
                    transaction.type !== "initial"
                      ? () => handleDeleteTransaction(transaction.id)
                      : undefined
                  }
                  key={transaction.id}
                >
                  <TransactionItem
                    transaction={transaction}
                    account={account}
                  />
                </SwipeableElement>
              );
            })
          )}
        </div>
        {!isLoading && (
          <Button href="/transactions">مشاهده همه تراکنش‌ها</Button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== app/splash/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import Image from "next/image";
import { SplashShownKey } from "@/utility/keys/sessionStore";

export default function Splash() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const fromPath = searchParams.get("from") || "/";

  useEffect(() => {
    async function load() {
      try {
        const authRes = await fetch("/api/auth/me");
        if (authRes.status === 401) {
          const currentUrl = window.location.origin + fromPath;
          router.replace(
            `https://auth.rezakelidari.ir/?redirect=${encodeURIComponent(currentUrl)}`,
          );
          return;
        }

        const initRes = await fetch("/api/init");

        if (initRes.status === 200) {
          sessionStorage.setItem(SplashShownKey, "1");
          router.replace(fromPath);
          return;
        }

        if (initRes.status === 204) {
          // ✅ دیگر نیازی به محاسبه timestamp در کلاینت نیست
          const createRes = await fetch("/api/init", { method: "POST" });

          if (createRes.status === 200) {
            sessionStorage.setItem(SplashShownKey, "1");
            router.replace(fromPath);
            return;
          } else {
            console.error("Failed to initialize default accounts");
          }
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Splash load error:", error);
      }
    }

    const timer = setTimeout(load, 1000);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [router, fromPath]);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col place-content-center items-center h-screen relative">
      <Image src="/logo.svg" alt="Logo" height={50} width={50} />
      <br />
      <p className="font-extralight">مدیریت مالی شخصی جیب</p>
      <span className="text-secondary absolute bottom-10 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 text-xs">
        درحال بارگذاری ...
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

===== app/transactions/add/account/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import AccountsListElement from "@/components/AccountsListElement";
import NavbarTitle from "@/components/NavbarTitle";
import useBalances from "@/utility/balance/service/useBalances";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY } from "../page";
import { TransactionType } from "@/utility/transactions/types";
import { AccountType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";
import { getAll } from "@/utility/db/Database";
import { DatabaseStores } from "@/utility/keys/database";

export default function AccountSelector() {
  const { getBalances } = useBalances();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [accounts, setAccounts] = useState<AccountType[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true); 
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const { accountsStore } = DatabaseStores;

  const accountParam = searchParams.get("type") as
    | "toAccount"
    | "fromAccount"
    | null;

  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string>();
  const [transactionType, setTransactionType] = useState<
    TransactionType["type"] | null
  >(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const data = sessionStorage.getItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY);
    if (!data) return;

    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(data) as TransactionType;
      setTransactionType(parsed.type);

      const currentSelected =
        accountParam === "fromAccount"
          ? parsed.credit
          : parsed.type === "credit"
            ? parsed.credit
            : parsed.debit;
      setSelectedId(currentSelected || "");
    } catch (e) {}
  }, [accountParam]);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try {
        setAccounts(
          (await getAll<AccountType>(accountsStore)).sort(
            (a, b) => a.order - b.order,
          ),
        );
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    })();
  }, [accountsStore]);

  function handleSelect(id: string): void {
    const data = sessionStorage.getItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY);
    if (!data) {
      router.back();
      return;
    }

    try {
      const currentTransaction = JSON.parse(data) as TransactionType;
      let update: Partial<TransactionType> = {};

      if (currentTransaction.type === "transfer") {
        update =
          accountParam === "fromAccount" ? { credit: id } : { debit: id };
      } else if (currentTransaction.type === "debit") {
        update = { debit: id };
      } else if (currentTransaction.type === "credit") {
        update = { credit: id };
      }

      const newData = JSON.stringify({ ...currentTransaction, ...update });
      sessionStorage.setItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY, newData);
    } catch (e) {}

    router.back();
  }

  let navbarTitle = "انتخاب حساب";
  if (
    transactionType === "debit" ||
    (transactionType === "transfer" && accountParam === "toAccount")
  ) {
    navbarTitle = "حساب مقصد";
  } else if (
    transactionType === "credit" ||
    (transactionType === "transfer" && accountParam === "fromAccount")
  ) {
    navbarTitle = "حساب مبدأ";
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-7.5 grid gap-15 w-screen overflow-hidden animate-fade-in">
      <div className="grid gap-7.5">
        <NavbarTitle title={navbarTitle} disabled />
      </div>
      <div className="grid gap-4">
        {isLoading ? (
          <>
            <div className="h-16 w-full bg-elements-background border border-elements-border rounded-2xl animate-pulse"></div>
            <div className="h-16 w-full bg-elements-background border border-elements-border rounded-2xl animate-pulse"></div>
            <div className="h-16 w-full bg-elements-background border border-elements-border rounded-2xl animate-pulse"></div>
            <div className="h-16 w-full bg-elements-background border border-elements-border rounded-2xl animate-pulse"></div>
          </>
        ) : (
          accounts.map((item) => {
            const isSelected = selectedId === item.id;
            const isTransfer = transactionType === "transfer";

            return (
              (!item.hidden || isTransfer) && (
                <AccountsListElement
                  key={item.id}
                  onClick={() => handleSelect(item.id)}
                  account={item}
                  balances={getBalances()}
                  desaturated={!!selectedId && !isSelected}
                />
              )
            );
          })
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== app/transactions/add/category/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import NavbarTitle from "@/components/NavbarTitle";
import { TransactionType } from "@/utility/transactions/types";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY } from "../page";
import { TransactionCategories } from "@/utility/shared/transactionCategories";
import Title from "@/components/SectionTitle";

export default function CategorySelector() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const accountParam = searchParams.get("type") as "debit" | "credit" | null;
  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<number>();
  const [transactionType, setTransactionType] = useState<
    TransactionType["type"] | null
  >(null);
  const router = useRouter();

  const itemRefs = useRef<Map<number, HTMLDivElement>>(new Map());

  const categories =
    transactionType === "debit"
      ? TransactionCategories.income
      : TransactionCategories.expense;

  useEffect(() => {
    const data = sessionStorage.getItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY);
    if (!data) return;

    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(data) as TransactionType;
      setTransactionType(parsed.type);

      const currentSelected = Number(
        accountParam === "debit" ? parsed.credit : parsed.debit,
      );
      setSelectedCategory(currentSelected || 0);
    } catch (e) {}
  }, [accountParam]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!!selectedCategory && selectedCategory !== 0) {
      const node = itemRefs.current.get(selectedCategory);
      if (node) {
        node.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "center" });
      }
    }
  }, [selectedCategory, categories]);

  function handleSelect(id: string): void {
    const data = sessionStorage.getItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY);
    if (!data) {
      router.back();
      return;
    }

    try {
      const currentTransaction = JSON.parse(data) as TransactionType;
      let update: Partial<TransactionType> = {};

      if (currentTransaction.type === "debit") {
        update = { credit: id };
      } else if (currentTransaction.type === "credit") {
        update = { debit: id };
      }

      const newData = JSON.stringify({ ...currentTransaction, ...update });
      sessionStorage.setItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY, newData);
    } catch (e) {}

    router.back();
  }

  if (!accountParam) return null;

  return (
    <div className="p-7.5 grid gap-15 w-screen overflow-hidden">
      <div className="grid gap-7.5">
        <NavbarTitle title="انتخاب دسته‌بندی" />
      </div>
      <div className="grid gap-15">
        {categories.map((items) => (
          <div key={items.id} className="grid gap-7.5">
            <Title>{items.name}</Title>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2.5 w-full">
              {items.sub.map((sub) => {
                const isSelected = selectedCategory === sub.id;

                return (
                  <div
                    key={sub.id}
                    ref={(el) => {
                      if (el) itemRefs.current.set(sub.id, el);
                      else itemRefs.current.delete(sub.id);
                    }}
                    onClick={() => handleSelect(sub.id.toString())}
                    className={`flex flex-col gap-2 items-center justify-center text-xs rounded-2xl p-2 cursor-pointer transition-all
                    ${
                      transactionType === "debit"
                        ? "bg-income-background border border-income-border"
                        : "bg-expense-background border border-expense-border"
                    }
                    ${!!selectedCategory && !isSelected ? "saturate-25 opacity-75" : ""}
                    ${isSelected ? "scale-110 shadow-lg shadow-zinc-200" : ""}`}
                  >
                    <sub.icon
                      height={40}
                      width={40}
                      className={
                        transactionType === "debit"
                          ? "fill-income-primary"
                          : "fill-expense-primary"
                      }
                    />
                    <span
                      className={`text-[9px] ${
                        transactionType === "debit"
                          ? "text-income-primary"
                          : "text-expense-primary"
                      } text-center`}
                    >
                      {sub.name}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== app/transactions/add/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import Capsule from "@/components/Capsule";
import NavbarTitle from "@/components/NavbarTitle";
import Left from "@/icons/Left";
import { TransactionType } from "@/utility/transactions/types";
import { AccountType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";
import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname, useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { getCategoryById } from "@/utility/shared/transactionsMethods";
import {
  formatJalaliDate,
  formatTimeInput,
  formatJalaliInput,
  formatTimeTyping,
  parseJalaliInput,
  parseTimeInput,
  mergeDateTime,
} from "@/utility/shared/formatters";
import { usePageShowSync } from "@/utility/shared/usePageShowSync";
import { toEnglishDigits } from "@/utility/shared/timeMethods";
import { getAll, putItem } from "@/utility/db/Database";
import { TagType } from "@/utility/shared/tagTypes";
import { DatabaseStores } from "@/utility/keys/database";

export const CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY = "new-transaction";

const defaultTransaction: TransactionType = {
  id: crypto.randomUUID(),
  type: "credit",
  name: "",
  time: Date.now(),
  value: 0,
  debit: "",
  credit: "",
  note: "",
};

export default function AddTransaction() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const transactionType = searchParams.get("type") as TransactionType["type"];
  const router = useRouter();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [tags, setTags] = useState<TagType[]>([]);
  const [accounts, setAccounts] = useState<AccountType[]>([]);
  const { accountsStore, transactionsStore, tagsStore } = DatabaseStores;

  const [transaction, setTransaction] = useState<TransactionType>(() => {
    if (typeof window === "undefined")
      return { ...defaultTransaction, id: "", type: transactionType };

    const data = sessionStorage.getItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY);
    if (!data)
      return {
        ...defaultTransaction,
        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
        type: transactionType,
      };

    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(data) as Partial<TransactionType>;
      const id =
        parsed.id && parsed.id !== "" ? parsed.id : crypto.randomUUID();
      return { ...defaultTransaction, ...parsed, id, type: transactionType };
    } catch {
      return {
        ...defaultTransaction,
        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
        type: transactionType,
      };
    }
  });

  const [dateInput, setDateInput] = useState(() =>
    formatJalaliDate(transaction.time),
  );

  const [timeInput, setTimeInput] = useState(() =>
    formatTimeInput(transaction.time),
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadInitialData() {
      if (!transactionType) {
        router.replace("/");
        return;
      }

      const accs = await getAll<AccountType>(accountsStore);
      setAccounts(accs);
      setTags(await getAll<TagType>(tagsStore));

      if (accs.length === 0) {
        router.replace(
          `/splash?from=/transactions/add?type=${transactionType}`,
        );
      }
    }
    loadInitialData();
  }, [router, transactionType]);

  const toAccountName = useMemo(() => {
    const id =
      transaction.type === "credit" ? transaction.credit : transaction.debit;
    return accounts.find((item) => item.id === id)?.name ?? "";
  }, [accounts, transaction.type, transaction.credit, transaction.debit]);

  const fromAccountName = useMemo(() => {
    if (transaction.type !== "transfer") return "";
    return accounts.find((item) => item.id === transaction.credit)?.name ?? "";
  }, [accounts, transaction.type, transaction.credit]);

  const tagName = useMemo(() => {
    if (transaction.tag)
      return tags.find((item) => item.id == transaction.tag)?.name;
    else return null;
  }, [transaction.tag, tags]);

  const categoryName = useMemo(() => {
    const id =
      transaction.type === "debit"
        ? transaction.credit
        : transaction.type === "credit"
          ? transaction.debit
          : 0;
    return id ? (getCategoryById(id)?.name ?? "") : "";
  }, [transaction.type, transaction.credit, transaction.debit]);

  const isValid = useMemo(() => {
    const nameValid = transaction.name.trim().length >= 3;
    const valueValid = transaction.value > 0;
    const dateValid = dateInput.trim().length === 10;
    const timeValid = timeInput.trim().length === 5;
    let accountAndCategoryValid = false;
    if (transaction.type === "transfer") {
      accountAndCategoryValid =
        !!transaction.debit &&
        !!transaction.credit &&
        transaction.debit !== transaction.credit;
    } else if (transaction.type === "debit") {
      accountAndCategoryValid = !!transaction.debit && !!transaction.credit;
    } else if (transaction.type === "credit") {
      accountAndCategoryValid = !!transaction.credit && !!transaction.debit;
    }
    return (
      nameValid &&
      valueValid &&
      dateValid &&
      timeValid &&
      accountAndCategoryValid
    );
  }, [transaction, dateInput, timeInput]);

  const persist = useCallback((data: TransactionType) => {
    sessionStorage.setItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
  }, []);

  const updateTransaction = useCallback(
    (updated: Partial<TransactionType>) => {
      setTransaction((prev) => {
        const newTransaction = { ...prev, ...updated };
        persist(newTransaction);
        return newTransaction;
      });
    },
    [persist],
  );

  const resetFormState = useCallback(() => {
    const cleared: TransactionType = {
      ...defaultTransaction,
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      type: transactionType,
      time: Date.now(),
    };
    setTransaction(cleared);
    setDateInput(formatJalaliDate(cleared.time));
    setTimeInput(formatTimeInput(cleared.time));
  }, [transactionType]);

  const handleNameChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) =>
    updateTransaction({ name: e.target.value });

  const handleValueChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const raw = toEnglishDigits(e.target.value).replace(/\D/g, "");
    updateTransaction({ value: Number(raw || 0) });
  };

  const handleDateChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const formatted = formatJalaliInput(e.target.value, dateInput);
    setDateInput(formatted);
    const timestamp = parseJalaliInput(formatted);
    if (timestamp > 0) {
      updateTransaction({
        time: mergeDateTime(transaction.time, timestamp, "date"),
      });
    }
  };

  const handleTimeChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const formatted = formatTimeTyping(e.target.value, timeInput);
    setTimeInput(formatted);
    const timestamp = parseTimeInput(formatted);
    if (timestamp > 0) {
      updateTransaction({
        time: mergeDateTime(transaction.time, timestamp, "time"),
      });
    }
  };

  const handleNoteChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) =>
    updateTransaction({ note: e.target.value });

  const handleSave = async () => {
    if (!isValid) return;
    await putItem(transactionsStore, transaction);
    sessionStorage.removeItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY);
    resetFormState();
    router.back();
  };

  const handleCancel = () => {
    sessionStorage.removeItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY);
    resetFormState();
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    persist(transaction);
  }, []); // eslint-disable-line

  usePageShowSync(() => {
    const data = sessionStorage.getItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY);
    if (!data) return;
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(data) as TransactionType;
      const corrected: TransactionType = { ...parsed, type: transactionType };
      setTransaction((prev) => {
        if (JSON.stringify(prev) === JSON.stringify(corrected)) return prev;
        setDateInput(formatJalaliDate(corrected.time));
        setTimeInput(formatTimeInput(corrected.time));
        return corrected;
      });
    } catch {}
  }, [transactionType]);

  if (!transactionType) return null;

  return (
    <div className="p-7.5 grid gap-15">
      <div className="grid gap-7.5">
        <NavbarTitle
          title={
            transactionType === "debit"
              ? "دریافت جدید"
              : transactionType === "credit"
                ? "پرداخت جدید"
                : "انتقال جدید"
          }
          onClick={handleCancel}
        >
          <Capsule Icon={Left} primary={isValid} onClick={handleSave}>
            ذخیره تراکنش
          </Capsule>
        </NavbarTitle>
      </div>
      <div className="grid gap-7.5">
        {transactionType !== "transfer" && (
          <div className="grid gap-2">
            <span className="text-xs">نام تراکنش</span>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={transaction.name}
              onChange={handleNameChange}
              placeholder={
                transactionType === "debit"
                  ? "حقوق ماهانه"
                  : transactionType === "credit"
                    ? "خرید خونه"
                    : "انتقال بین بانکی"
              }
              className="h-12 text-sm border border-elements-border bg-elements-background px-4 rounded-2xl outline-none focus:bg-primary-background focus:border-primary-border transition-all"
            />
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="grid gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs">مبلغ</span>
          <div className="relative">
            <input
              type="text"
              inputMode="numeric"
              value={
                transaction.value
                  ? transaction.value.toLocaleString("fa-IR")
                  : ""
              }
              onChange={handleValueChange}
              placeholder="۰"
              className="h-12 w-full text-sm border border-elements-border bg-elements-background px-4 pl-10 rounded-2xl outline-none focus:bg-primary-background focus:border-primary-border transition-all"
            />
            <span className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-secondary text-[10px] pointer-events-none">
              ﷼
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
        {transactionType === "transfer" && (
          <div className="grid gap-2">
            <span className="text-xs">حساب مبدأ</span>
            <Link
              href={`${pathname}/account?type=fromAccount`}
              className="flex items-center justify-between h-12 w-full border border-elements-border bg-elements-background px-4 rounded-2xl text-sm active:bg-elements-background/70 transition-colors"
            >
              <span>{fromAccountName || "انتخاب حساب مبدأ"}</span>
              <Left width={16} height={16} className="fill-secondary" />
            </Link>
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="grid gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs">
            {transactionType === "transfer" ? "حساب مقصد" : "حساب"}
          </span>
          <Link
            href={`${pathname}/account?type=toAccount`}
            className="flex items-center justify-between h-12 w-full border border-elements-border bg-elements-background px-4 rounded-2xl text-sm active:bg-elements-background/70 transition-colors"
          >
            <span>
              {toAccountName ||
                (transactionType === "transfer"
                  ? "انتخاب حساب مقصد"
                  : "انتخاب حساب")}
            </span>
            <Left width={16} height={16} className="fill-secondary" />
          </Link>
        </div>
        {["debit", "credit"].includes(transactionType) && (
          <div className="grid gap-2">
            <span className="text-xs">دسته‌بندی</span>
            <Link
              href={`${pathname}/category?type=${transactionType}`}
              className="flex items-center justify-between h-12 w-full border border-elements-border bg-elements-background px-4 rounded-2xl text-sm active:bg-elements-background/70 transition-colors"
            >
              <span>{categoryName || "انتخاب دسته‌بندی"}</span>
              <Left width={16} height={16} className="fill-secondary" />
            </Link>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="grid gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs">برچسب (اختیاری)</span>
          <Link
            href={tags.length ? `${pathname}/tag` : "#"}
            className="flex items-center justify-between h-12 w-full border border-elements-border bg-elements-background px-4 rounded-2xl text-sm active:bg-elements-background/70 transition-colors"
          >
            <span>{tagName ?? "نام برچسب"}</span>
            <Left width={16} height={16} className="fill-secondary" />
          </Link>
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs">تاریخ</span>
          <input
            type="text"
            inputMode="numeric"
            value={dateInput}
            onChange={handleDateChange}
            placeholder="۱۴۰۵/۰۱/۰۱"
            className="h-12 text-sm border border-elements-border bg-elements-background px-4 rounded-2xl outline-none focus:bg-primary-background focus:border-primary-border transition-all"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="grid gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs">ساعت</span>
          <input
            type="text"
            inputMode="numeric"
            value={timeInput}
            onChange={handleTimeChange}
            placeholder="۱۲:۰۰"
            className="h-12 text-sm border border-elements-border bg-elements-background px-4 rounded-2xl outline-none focus:bg-primary-background focus:border-primary-border transition-all"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="grid gap-7.5">
          <div className="grid gap-2">
            <span className="text-xs">شرح تراکنش (اختیاری)</span>
            <textarea
              value={transaction.note}
              onChange={handleNoteChange}
              placeholder="توضیحات"
              className="min-h-25 resize-y text-sm border border-elements-border bg-elements-background p-4 rounded-2xl outline-none focus:bg-primary-background focus:border-primary-border transition-all"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <br />
    </div>
  );
}

===== app/transactions/add/tag/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import ListElement from "@/components/ListElement";
import NavbarTitle from "@/components/NavbarTitle";
import Tag from "@/icons/Tag";
import { PrimaryColors } from "@/utility/shared/colors";
import { getAll } from "@/utility/db/Database";
import { TagType } from "@/utility/shared/tagTypes";
import { TransactionType } from "@/utility/transactions/types";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY } from "../page";

export default function TagSelector() {
  const [tags, setTags] = useState<TagType[]>([]);
  const router = useRouter();
  const [selectedTag, setSelectedTag] = useState<string | undefined>();

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      const data = sessionStorage.getItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY)!;

      const tx = JSON.parse(data) as TransactionType;
      setSelectedTag(tx.tag);

      setTags(await getAll<TagType>("tags"));
    })();
  }, [router]);

  function handleSelect(id: string): void {
    const data = sessionStorage.getItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY);
    if (!data) {
      router.back();
      return;
    }

    try {
      const tx = JSON.parse(data) as TransactionType;
      const newData = JSON.stringify({ ...tx, tag: id });
      sessionStorage.setItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY, newData);
    } catch (e) {}

    router.back();
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-7.5 grid gap-15 w-screen overflow-hidden">
      <div className="grid gap-7.5">
        <NavbarTitle title="انتخاب برچسب" />
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4">
        {!!selectedTag && selectedTag !== "" && (
          <ListElement
            iconColor={PrimaryColors.primary}
            Icon={Tag}
            href="٫"
            onClick={() => handleSelect("")}
          >
            حذف برچسب
          </ListElement>
        )}

        {tags.map((item) => (
          <ListElement
            iconColor={PrimaryColors.primary}
            Icon={Tag}
            primary={selectedTag === item.id}
            href="#"
            key={item.id}
            onClick={() => handleSelect(item.id)}
          >
            {item.name}
          </ListElement>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
