===== app/transactions/detail/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import Box from "@/components/Box";
import Capsule from "@/components/Capsule";
import NavbarTitle from "@/components/NavbarTitle";
import { AccountColors } from "@/utility/shared/colors";
import { getCategoryById } from "@/utility/shared/transactionsMethods";
import Pen from "@/icons/Pen";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { ComponentProps, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { TagType } from "@/utility/shared/tagTypes";
import { getAll, getItem } from "@/utility/db/Database";
import { AccountType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";
import { TransactionType } from "@/utility/transactions/types";
import { DatabaseStores } from "@/utility/keys/database";

export default function TransactionDetail() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const [transaction, setTransaction] = useState<TransactionType>();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [tagName, setTagName] = useState<string>("");
  const { accountsStore, transactionsStore, tagsStore } = DatabaseStores;
  const [account, setAccount] = useState<AccountType>();
  const [fromAccount, setFromAccount] = useState<AccountType>();
  const [toAccount, setToAccount] = useState<AccountType>();

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  const txID = searchParams.get("id") ?? "";

  const isTxDebit =
    transaction?.type === "debit" || transaction?.type === "initial";
  const categoryID = isTxDebit ? transaction?.credit : transaction?.debit;
  const txCategory = getCategoryById(categoryID ?? "");

  const txName =
    transaction?.type === "initial" ? "موجودی اولیه" : transaction?.name;

  const txTime = Number(transaction?.time) || 0;
  const txDate = new Date(txTime);
  const txValue = Number(transaction?.value) || 0;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!txID) {
      router.back();
      return;
    }

    (async () => {
      try {
        const tx = (await getItem(transactionsStore, txID)) as TransactionType;
        if (!tx) return;

        setTransaction(tx);

        const accountID = tx.type === "credit" ? tx.credit : tx.debit;

        const promises = [
          getItem(accountsStore, accountID),
          getItem(accountsStore, tx.credit),
          getItem(accountsStore, tx.debit),
        ];

        if (tx.tag) {
          promises.push(getItem<TagType>(tagsStore, tx.tag));
        }

        const results = await Promise.all(promises);

        setAccount(results[0] as AccountType);
        setFromAccount(results[1] as AccountType);
        setToAccount(results[2] as AccountType);

        if (tx.tag && results[3]) {
          setTagName((results[3] as TagType).name);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Failed to load transaction details:", error);
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    })();
  }, [txID, router, accountsStore, transactionsStore, tagsStore]);

  if (isLoading || !transaction) {
    return (
      <div className="p-7.5 grid gap-15">
        <div className="grid gap-7.5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <div className="h-5 w-5 bg-elements-border rounded-md animate-pulse"></div>
              <div className="h-6 w-32 bg-elements-border rounded-md animate-pulse"></div>
            </div>
            <div className="h-8 w-24 bg-elements-border rounded-4xl animate-pulse"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Box className="animate-pulse">
          <div className="h-6 w-1/3 mx-auto bg-elements-border rounded-md mb-8"></div>
          <div className="grid gap-5">
            <div className="h-4 w-full bg-elements-border rounded-md"></div>
            <div className="h-4 w-full bg-elements-border rounded-md"></div>
            <div className="h-4 w-2/3 bg-elements-border rounded-md"></div>
            <div className="h-20 w-full bg-elements-border rounded-2xl mt-4"></div>
          </div>
        </Box>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-7.5 grid gap-15 animate-fade-in">
      <div className="grid gap-7.5">
        <NavbarTitle title="مشاهده تراکنش">
          {transaction.type === "initial" ? (
            <Capsule
              href={`/accounts/edit?id=${transaction?.debit}`}
              Icon={Pen}
            >
              ویرایش حساب
            </Capsule>
          ) : (
            <Capsule
              href={`/transactions/edit?id=${transaction.id}`}
              Icon={Pen}
            >
              ویرایش
            </Capsule>
          )}
        </NavbarTitle>
      </div>

      <Box>
        <h1 className="text-center">{txName}</h1>
        <br />
        <div className="grid gap-5">
          <InfoRow
            name="مبلغ"
            className={`text-xs ${isTxDebit ? "text-income-primary" : transaction?.type === "credit" ? "text-expense-primary" : ""}`}
          >
            {`${txValue.toLocaleString("fa-ir")} ${isTxDebit ? "+" : transaction?.type === "credit" ? "-" : ""} ﷼`}
          </InfoRow>

          {txCategory && <InfoRow name="دسته‌بندی">{txCategory.name}</InfoRow>}

          {["debit", "credit", "initial"].includes(transaction?.type ?? "") ? (
            <InfoRow name="حساب" className="text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5">
              {account?.name}{" "}
              <span
                className={`p-1 rounded-4xl ${AccountColors[account?.color ?? "blue1"].primary}`}
              ></span>
            </InfoRow>
          ) : (
            <>
              <InfoRow
                name="حساب مقصد"
                className="text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5"
              >
                {toAccount?.name}{" "}
                <span
                  className={`p-1 rounded-4xl ${AccountColors[toAccount?.color ?? "blue1"].primary}`}
                ></span>
              </InfoRow>

              <InfoRow
                name="حساب مبدا"
                className="text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5"
              >
                {fromAccount?.name}{" "}
                <span
                  className={`p-1 rounded-4xl ${AccountColors[fromAccount?.color ?? "blue1"].primary}`}
                ></span>
              </InfoRow>
            </>
          )}

          <InfoRow name="برچسب" className="text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5">
            {tagName || "بدون برچسب"}
          </InfoRow>

          <InfoRow name="تاریخ" className="text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5">
            {txDate.toLocaleDateString("fa-ir", {
              year: "numeric",
              month: "2-digit",
              day: "2-digit",
            })}
          </InfoRow>

          <InfoRow name="ساعت" className="text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5">
            {txDate.toLocaleString("fa-ir", {
              hour: "2-digit",
              minute: "2-digit",
              second: "2-digit",
            })}
          </InfoRow>

          {transaction?.note && (
            <div className="p-4 min-h-25 bg-white rounded-2xl border-elements-border border text-[10px] text-justify">
              {transaction.note}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </Box>
    </div>
  );
}

type InfoRowPropsType = ComponentProps<"h3"> & {
  name: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
};

function InfoRow({ name, children, ...props }: InfoRowPropsType) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
      <h6 className="text-secondary text-[9px]">{name}</h6>
      <h3 className="text-xs" {...props}>
        {children}
      </h3>
    </div>
  );
}

و


===== app/transactions/edit/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import Capsule from "@/components/Capsule";
import NavbarTitle from "@/components/NavbarTitle";
import Left from "@/icons/Left";
import { TransactionType } from "@/utility/transactions/types";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { ChangeEvent, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { getCategoryById } from "@/utility/shared/transactionsMethods";
import {
  formatJalaliDate,
  formatTimeInput,
  formatJalaliInput,
  formatTimeTyping,
  parseJalaliInput,
  parseTimeInput,
  mergeDateTime,
} from "@/utility/shared/formatters";
import { usePageShowSync } from "@/utility/shared/usePageShowSync";
import { CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY } from "../add/page";
import { toEnglishDigits } from "@/utility/shared/timeMethods";
import { TagType } from "@/utility/shared/tagTypes";
import { getAll, getItem, putItem } from "@/utility/db/Database";
import { AccountType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";
import { DatabaseStores } from "@/utility/keys/database";

export default function TransactionEdit() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const txID = searchParams.get("id") ?? "";
  const router = useRouter();
  const { accountsStore, transactionsStore, tagsStore } = DatabaseStores;

  const [accounts, setAccounts] = useState<AccountType[]>([]);
  const [tags, setTags] = useState<TagType[]>([]);

  const [transaction, setTransaction] = useState<TransactionType | null>(null);
  const [dateInput, setDateInput] = useState("");
  const [timeInput, setTimeInput] = useState("");

  // ==================== State Update & Sync ====================
  const persist = useCallback((data: TransactionType) => {
    sessionStorage.setItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
  }, []);

  // ==================== Initial Data Load ====================
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!txID) {
      router.replace("/transactions");
      return;
    }

    async function loadInitialData() {
      try {
        const draftData = sessionStorage.getItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY);
        let initialTx: TransactionType | null = null;

        if (draftData) {
          try {
            const parsed = JSON.parse(draftData) as TransactionType;
            if (parsed.id === txID) initialTx = parsed;
          } catch {}
        }

        if (!initialTx) {
          initialTx =
            (await getItem<TransactionType>(transactionsStore, txID)) ?? null;
        }

        if (!initialTx) {
          router.replace("/transactions");
          return;
        }

        persist(initialTx);

        setTransaction(initialTx);
        setDateInput(formatJalaliDate(initialTx.time));
        setTimeInput(formatTimeInput(initialTx.time));

        const [accs, tgs] = await Promise.all([
          getAll<AccountType>(accountsStore),
          getAll<TagType>(tagsStore),
        ]);

        accs.sort((a, b) => (a.order ?? 0) - (b.order ?? 0));

        setAccounts(accs);
        setTags(tgs);
      } catch (error) {
        router.replace("/transactions");
      }
    }

    loadInitialData();
  }, [txID, router, accountsStore, transactionsStore, tagsStore, persist]);

  const transactionType = transaction?.type;

  // ==================== Derived States ====================
  const toAccountName = useMemo(() => {
    if (!transaction) return "";
    const id =
      transaction.type === "credit" ? transaction.credit : transaction.debit;
    return accounts.find((item) => item.id === id)?.name ?? "";
  }, [accounts, transaction]);

  const fromAccountName = useMemo(() => {
    if (!transaction || transaction.type !== "transfer") return "";
    return accounts.find((item) => item.id === transaction.credit)?.name ?? "";
  }, [accounts, transaction]);

  const tagName = useMemo(() => {
    if (!transaction || !transaction.tag) return "";
    return tags.find((item) => item.id === transaction.tag)?.name ?? "";
  }, [transaction, tags]);

  const categoryName = useMemo(() => {
    if (!transaction) return "";
    const id =
      transaction.type === "debit"
        ? transaction.credit
        : transaction.type === "credit"
          ? transaction.debit
          : 0;
    return id ? (getCategoryById(id)?.name ?? "") : "";
  }, [transaction]);

  // ==================== Validation ====================
  const isValid = useMemo(() => {
    if (!transaction) return false;
    const nameValid = transaction.name.trim().length >= 3;
    const valueValid = transaction.value > 0;
    const dateValid = dateInput.trim().length === 10;
    const timeValid = timeInput.trim().length === 5;
    let accountAndCategoryValid = false;
    if (transaction.type === "transfer") {
      accountAndCategoryValid =
        !!transaction.debit &&
        !!transaction.credit &&
        transaction.debit !== transaction.credit;
    } else if (transaction.type === "debit") {
      accountAndCategoryValid = !!transaction.debit && !!transaction.credit;
    } else if (transaction.type === "credit") {
      accountAndCategoryValid = !!transaction.credit && !!transaction.debit;
    }
    return (
      nameValid &&
      valueValid &&
      dateValid &&
      timeValid &&
      accountAndCategoryValid
    );
  }, [transaction, dateInput, timeInput]);

  const updateTransactionState = useCallback(
    (updated: Partial<TransactionType>) => {
      setTransaction((prev) => {
        if (!prev) return prev;
        const newTransaction = { ...prev, ...updated };
        persist(newTransaction);
        return newTransaction;
      });
    },
    [persist],
  );

  // ==================== Handlers ====================
  const handleNameChange = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) =>
    updateTransactionState({ name: e.target.value });

  const handleValueChange = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const raw = toEnglishDigits(e.target.value).replace(/\D/g, "");
    updateTransactionState({ value: Number(raw) || 0 });
  };

  const handleDateChange = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const formatted = formatJalaliInput(e.target.value, dateInput);
    setDateInput(formatted);
    const timestamp = parseJalaliInput(formatted);
    if (timestamp > 0 && transaction) {
      updateTransactionState({
        time: mergeDateTime(transaction.time, timestamp, "date"),
      });
    }
  };

  const handleTimeChange = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const formatted = formatTimeTyping(e.target.value, timeInput);
    setTimeInput(formatted);
    const timestamp = parseTimeInput(formatted);
    if (timestamp > 0 && transaction) {
      updateTransactionState({
        time: mergeDateTime(transaction.time, timestamp, "time"),
      });
    }
  };

  const handleNoteChange = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) =>
    updateTransactionState({ note: e.target.value });

  const handleSave = async () => {
    if (!isValid || !transaction) return;
    await putItem(transactionsStore, transaction);
    sessionStorage.removeItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY);
    router.back();
  };

  const handleCancel = () => {
    sessionStorage.removeItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY);
    router.back();
  };

  // ==================== Page Show Sync (برای برگشت از صفحات انتخاب) ====================
  usePageShowSync(() => {
    const data = sessionStorage.getItem(CREATE_TRANSACTION_KEY);
    if (!data || !txID) return;
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(data) as TransactionType;
      if (parsed.id !== txID) return;
      setTransaction((prev) => {
        if (!prev || JSON.stringify(prev) === JSON.stringify(parsed))
          return prev;
        setDateInput(formatJalaliDate(parsed.time));
        setTimeInput(formatTimeInput(parsed.time));
        return parsed;
      });
    } catch {}
  }, [txID]);

  if (!txID || !transaction || !transactionType) return null;

  return (
    <div className="p-7.5 grid gap-15">
      <div className="grid gap-7.5">
        <NavbarTitle
          title={
            transactionType === "debit"
              ? "ویرایش دریافت"
              : transactionType === "credit"
                ? "ویرایش پرداخت"
                : "ویرایش انتقال"
          }
          onClick={handleCancel}
        >
          <Capsule Icon={Left} primary={isValid} onClick={handleSave}>
            ذخیره تغییرات
          </Capsule>
        </NavbarTitle>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-7.5">
        <div className="grid gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs">نام تراکنش</span>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={transaction.name}
            onChange={handleNameChange}
            placeholder={
              transactionType === "debit"
                ? "حقوق ماهانه"
                : transactionType === "credit"
                  ? "خرید خونه"
                  : "انتقال بین بانکی"
            }
            className="h-12 text-sm border border-elements-border bg-elements-background px-4 rounded-2xl outline-none focus:bg-primary-background focus:border-primary-border transition-all"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs">مبلغ</span>
          <div className="relative">
            <input
              type="text"
              inputMode="numeric"
              value={
                transaction.value
                  ? transaction.value.toLocaleString("fa-IR")
                  : ""
              }
              onChange={handleValueChange}
              placeholder="۰"
              className="h-12 w-full text-sm border border-elements-border bg-elements-background px-4 pl-10 rounded-2xl outline-none focus:bg-primary-background focus:border-primary-border transition-all"
            />
            <span className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-secondary text-[10px] pointer-events-none">
              ﷼
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {transactionType === "transfer" && (
          <div className="grid gap-2">
            <span className="text-xs">حساب مبدأ</span>
            <Link
              href={`/transactions/add/account?type=fromAccount`}
              className="flex items-center justify-between h-12 w-full border border-elements-border bg-elements-background px-4 rounded-2xl text-sm active:bg-elements-background/70 transition-colors"
            >
              <span>{fromAccountName || "انتخاب حساب مبدأ"}</span>
              <Left width={16} height={16} className="fill-secondary" />
            </Link>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="grid gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs">
            {transactionType === "transfer" ? "حساب مقصد" : "انتخاب حساب"}
          </span>
          <Link
            href={`/transactions/add/account?type=toAccount`}
            className="flex items-center justify-between h-12 w-full border border-elements-border bg-elements-background px-4 rounded-2xl text-sm active:bg-elements-background/70 transition-colors"
          >
            <span>
              {toAccountName ||
                (transactionType === "transfer"
                  ? "انتخاب حساب مقصد"
                  : "انتخاب حساب")}
            </span>
            <Left width={16} height={16} className="fill-secondary" />
          </Link>
        </div>

        {["debit", "credit"].includes(transactionType) && (
          <div className="grid gap-2">
            <span className="text-xs">دسته‌بندی</span>
            <Link
              href={`/transactions/add/category?type=${transactionType}`}
              className="flex items-center justify-between h-12 w-full border border-elements-border bg-elements-background px-4 rounded-2xl text-sm active:bg-elements-background/70 transition-colors"
            >
              <span>{categoryName || "انتخاب دسته‌بندی"}</span>
              <Left width={16} height={16} className="fill-secondary" />
            </Link>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="grid gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs">برچسب (اختیاری)</span>
          <Link
            href={tags.length ? "/transactions/add/tag" : "#"}
            className="flex items-center justify-between h-12 w-full border border-elements-border bg-elements-background px-4 rounded-2xl text-sm active:bg-elements-background/70 transition-colors"
          >
            <span>{tagName || "نام برچسب"}</span>
            <Left width={16} height={16} className="fill-secondary" />
          </Link>
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs">تاریخ</span>
          <input
            type="text"
            inputMode="numeric"
            value={dateInput}
            onChange={handleDateChange}
            placeholder="۱۴۰۵/۰۱/۰۱"
            className="h-12 text-sm border border-elements-border bg-elements-background px-4 rounded-2xl outline-none focus:bg-primary-background focus:border-primary-border transition-all"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs">ساعت</span>
          <input
            type="text"
            inputMode="numeric"
            value={timeInput}
            onChange={handleTimeChange}
            placeholder="۱۲:۰۰"
            className="h-12 text-sm border border-elements-border bg-elements-background px-4 rounded-2xl outline-none focus:bg-primary-background focus:border-primary-border transition-all"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-7.5">
          <div className="grid gap-2">
            <span className="text-xs">شرح تراکنش (اختیاری)</span>
            <textarea
              value={transaction.note}
              onChange={handleNoteChange}
              placeholder="توضیحات"
              className="min-h-25 resize-y text-sm border border-elements-border bg-elements-background p-4 rounded-2xl outline-none focus:bg-primary-background focus:border-primary-border transition-all"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <br />
    </div>
  );
}

===== app/transactions/filter-account/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import Button from "@/components/Button";
import NavbarTitle from "@/components/NavbarTitle";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { filterToUrl, parseStringArray } from "../methods";
import AccountsListElement from "@/components/AccountsListElement";
import useBalances from "@/utility/balance/service/useBalances";
import { getAll } from "@/utility/db/Database";
import { AccountType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";
import { DatabaseStores } from "@/utility/keys/database";

export default function AccountFilter() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const router = useRouter();
  const { getBalances } = useBalances();
  const { accountsStore } = DatabaseStores;

  const [accounts, setAccounts] = useState<AccountType[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [accountFilter, setAccountFilter] = useState<string[]>([]);

  const transactionsFilter = {
    timeWindow: Number(searchParams.get("time") ?? 29),
    accounts: parseStringArray(searchParams.get("accounts")),
    categories: parseStringArray(searchParams.get("categories")),
    tags: parseStringArray(searchParams.get("tags")),
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    async function load() {
      const accs = (await getAll(accountsStore)) as AccountType[];
      if (accs.length === 0) {
        const currentPath = window.location.pathname + window.location.search;
        router.replace(`/splash?from=${encodeURIComponent(currentPath)}`);
        return;
      }
      accs.sort((a, b) => (a.order ?? 0) - (b.order ?? 0));
      setAccounts(accs);
      setAccountFilter(transactionsFilter.accounts);
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
    load();
  }, [accountsStore, router]);

  const handleApplyFilter = () => {
    const selectedAccounts =
      accountFilter.length === 0
        ? accounts.map((item) => item.id)
        : accountFilter;

    const newUrl = `/transactions${filterToUrl({
      ...transactionsFilter,
      accounts:
        selectedAccounts.length !== accounts.length ? selectedAccounts : [],
    })}`;

    router.replace(newUrl);
  };

  if (isLoading) return null;

  return (
    <div className="p-7.5 grid gap-15 w-screen overflow-hidden">
      <div className="grid gap-7.5">
        <NavbarTitle title="حساب‌ها" disabled />
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4">
        {accounts.map((item) => (
          <AccountsListElement
            account={item}
            balances={getBalances()}
            key={item.id}
            disabled={!accountFilter.includes(item.id)}
            onClick={() => {
              setAccountFilter((prev) => {
                if (prev.includes(item.id))
                  return prev.filter((id) => id !== item.id);
                return [...prev, item.id];
              });
            }}
          />
        ))}
      </div>

      <Button onClick={handleApplyFilter}>اعمال فیلتر</Button>
    </div>
  );
}

===== app/transactions/filter-time/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import NavbarTitle from "@/components/NavbarTitle";
import { toJalaliDateArray } from "@/utility/shared/timeMethods";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { filterToUrl, parseStringArray, timeFilters } from "../methods";
import { PrimaryColors } from "@/utility/shared/colors";

export default function TimeFilter() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const router = useRouter();
  const date = new Date();
  const dateOptions: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions = {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "long",
  };
  const today = date.toLocaleDateString("fa-ir", dateOptions);

  const transactionsFilter = {
    timeWindow: Number(searchParams.get("time") ?? 29) || 29,
    accounts: parseStringArray(searchParams.get("accounts")),
    categories: parseStringArray(searchParams.get("categories")),
    tags: parseStringArray(searchParams.get("tags")),
  };

  const handleSelectTime = (time: number) => {
    const newUrl = `/transactions${filterToUrl({
      ...transactionsFilter,
      timeWindow: time,
    })}`;

    router.replace(newUrl);
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-7.5 grid gap-15 w-screen overflow-hidden">
      <div className="grid gap-7.5">
        <NavbarTitle title="بازه زمانی" />
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-5">
        {timeFilters(toJalaliDateArray(date)).map((item, index) => {
          const secondDay =
            item.time === 36500
              ? "بدون محدودیت زمانی"
              : new Date(
                  Date.now() - item.time * 1000 * 3600 * 24,
                ).toLocaleDateString("fa-ir", dateOptions);
          return (
            <div
              key={index}
              onClick={() => handleSelectTime(item.time)}
              className={`${transactionsFilter.timeWindow === item.time ? PrimaryColors.primary.box : PrimaryColors.secondary.box} px-5 py-4 rounded-2xl text-sm flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer`}
            >
              {item.name}
              <span className="text-xs text-secondary">
                {secondDay} {item.time !== 36500 && "~ " + today}
              </span>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

و


===== app/transactions/methods.ts =====

import { pastDaysInYear } from "@/utility/shared/timeMethods";

export type TransactionsFilterType = {
  timeWindow: number;
  accounts: string[];
  categories: string[];
  tags: string[];
};

export const filterToUrl = (filter: TransactionsFilterType): string => {
  const { timeWindow, accounts, categories, tags } = filter;
  let result = "?";

  result += `time=${timeWindow}&`;
  if (accounts && accounts.length > 0)
    result += `accounts=${accounts.join(",")}&`;
  if (categories && categories.length > 0)
    result += `categories=${categories.join(",")}&`;
  if (tags && tags.length > 0) result += `tags=${tags.join(",")}&`;

  return result;
};

export const parseStringArray = (param: string | null): string[] => {
  if (!param) return [];
  return param.split(",").filter((s) => s !== "");
};

export const timeFilters = (dateArray: number[]) => [
  { name: "۷ روز گذشته", time: 7 - 1 },
  { name: "۳۰ روز گذشته", time: 30 - 1 },
  { name: "این ماه", time: dateArray[2] - 1 },
  { name: "۹۰ روز گذشته", time: 90 - 1 },
  { name: "امسال", time: pastDaysInYear(dateArray) - 1 },
  { name: "از ابتدا", time: 36500 },
];

===== app/transactions/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import Capsule from "@/components/Capsule";
import NavbarTitle from "@/components/NavbarTitle";
import TransactionItem from "@/components/TransactionItem";
import TransactionItemLoading from "@/components/TransactionItemLoading";
import Left from "@/icons/Left";
import { usePathname, useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  filterToUrl,
  parseStringArray,
  timeFilters,
  TransactionsFilterType,
} from "./methods";
import { toJalaliDateArray } from "@/utility/shared/timeMethods";
import { AccountColors } from "@/utility/shared/colors";
import Description from "@/components/Description";
import Filter from "@/icons/Filter";
import Button from "@/components/Button";
import { AccountType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";
import { TransactionType } from "@/utility/transactions/types";
import { deleteItem, getAll } from "@/utility/db/Database";
import { DatabaseStores } from "@/utility/keys/database";
import SwipeableElement from "@/components/SwipeableElement";

export type transactionsFilterType = {
  timeWindow: number;
  accounts: string[];
  categories: string[];
  tags: string[];
};

export default function Transactions() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [transactions, setTransactions] = useState<TransactionType[]>([]);
  const [accounts, setAccounts] = useState<AccountType[]>([]);
  const timeFilter = timeFilters(toJalaliDateArray(new Date()));
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const { accountsStore, transactionsStore } = DatabaseStores;
  const [swipedItem, setSwipedItem] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  const transactionsFilter: TransactionsFilterType = useMemo(
    () => ({
      timeWindow: Number(searchParams.get("time") ?? 29),
      accounts: parseStringArray(searchParams.get("accounts")),
      categories: parseStringArray(searchParams.get("categories")),
      tags: parseStringArray(searchParams.get("tags")),
    }),
    [searchParams],
  );

  const filteredTransactions = useMemo(() => {
    const now = Date.now();
    const timeLimit = now - transactionsFilter.timeWindow * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

    return transactions
      .filter((transaction) => {
        if (transaction.time < timeLimit) return false;

        if (
          transactionsFilter.accounts.length > 0 &&
          !transactionsFilter.accounts.includes(transaction.credit) &&
          !transactionsFilter.accounts.includes(transaction.debit)
        ) {
          return false;
        }

        if (
          transactionsFilter.categories.length > 0 &&
          !transactionsFilter.categories.includes(transaction.debit) &&
          !transactionsFilter.categories.includes(transaction.credit)
        ) {
          return false;
        }

        if (
          transactionsFilter.tags.length > 0 &&
          (transaction.tag === undefined ||
            !transactionsFilter.tags.includes(transaction.tag))
        ) {
          return false;
        }

        return true;
      })
      .sort((a, b) => b.time - a.time);
  }, [transactions, transactionsFilter]);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try {
        setTransactions(await getAll<TransactionType>(transactionsStore));
        setAccounts(await getAll<AccountType>(accountsStore));
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Failed to load transactions:", error);
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    })();
  }, [accountsStore, transactionsStore]);

  return (
    <div className="p-7.5 flex flex-col gap-15 w-screen overflow-hidden min-h-screen animate-fade-in">
      <div className="grid gap-7.5">
        <NavbarTitle title="تراکنش‌ها" />
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-scroll scroll-auto w-fill scrollbar-none">
          <span className="me-2">فیلترها</span>
          <Capsule
            Icon={Left}
            href={`/transactions/filter-time${filterToUrl(transactionsFilter)}`}
            replace
            primary={transactionsFilter.timeWindow !== 29}
          >
            {timeFilter.find(
              (filter) => filter.time === transactionsFilter.timeWindow,
            )?.name ?? "۳۰ روز گذشته"}
          </Capsule>

          <Capsule
            Icon={Left}
            href={`/transactions/filter-account${filterToUrl(transactionsFilter)}`}
            replace
            primary={transactionsFilter.accounts.length > 0}
            accountColor={
              transactionsFilter.accounts.length === 1
                ? accounts.find(
                    (item) => item.id === transactionsFilter.accounts[0],
                  )?.color
                : undefined
            }
            className={
              transactionsFilter.accounts.length === 1
                ? `${
                    AccountColors[
                      accounts.find(
                        (item) => item.id === transactionsFilter.accounts[0],
                      )?.color ?? "black"
                    ].border
                  }
                  ${
                    AccountColors[
                      accounts.find(
                        (item) => item.id === transactionsFilter.accounts[0],
                      )?.color ?? "black"
                    ].text
                  }
                  ${
                    AccountColors[
                      accounts.find(
                        (item) => item.id === transactionsFilter.accounts[0],
                      )?.color ?? "black"
                    ].surface
                  }`
                : ""
            }
          >
            {transactionsFilter.accounts.length === 0
              ? "همه حساب‌ها"
              : transactionsFilter.accounts.length === 1
                ? accounts.find(
                    (item) => item.id === transactionsFilter.accounts[0],
                  )?.name
                : `${transactionsFilter.accounts.length.toLocaleString("fa-ir")} حساب`}
          </Capsule>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ✅ بخش نمایش لودینگ یا لیست تراکنش‌ها */}
      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="grid gap-4">
          <TransactionItemLoading />
          <TransactionItemLoading />
          <TransactionItemLoading />
          <TransactionItemLoading />
          <TransactionItemLoading />
        </div>
      ) : filteredTransactions.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-10 h-full">
          <Filter className="fill-secondary" height={75} width={75} />
          <Description>با فیلتر‌های انتخابی هیچ تراکنشی یافت نشد.</Description>
          {transactionsFilter.accounts.length > 0 ||
          transactionsFilter.categories.length > 0 ||
          transactionsFilter.tags.length > 0 ||
          transactionsFilter.timeWindow < 29 ? (
            <Button onClick={() => router.replace(pathname)}>
              حذف همه فیلتر‌ها
            </Button>
          ) : (
            (transactionsFilter.accounts.length > 0 ||
              transactionsFilter.categories.length > 0 ||
              transactionsFilter.tags.length > 0 ||
              transactionsFilter.timeWindow >= 29) && (
              <Button onClick={() => router.replace(`${pathname}?time=36500`)}>
                نمایش همه تراکنش‌ها
              </Button>
            )
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-4">
          {filteredTransactions.map((transaction) => {
            const accountID =
              transaction.type === "credit"
                ? transaction.credit
                : transaction.debit;
            const account = accounts.find((acc) => acc.id === accountID);

            if (!account) return null;

            return (
              <SwipeableElement
                isOpen={transaction.id === swipedItem}
                onOpenChange={() => setSwipedItem(transaction.id)}
                onCloseOthers={() => setSwipedItem(null)}
                onEdit={() =>
                  transaction.type !== "initial"
                    ? router.push(`/transactions/edit?id=${transaction.id}`)
                    : router.push(`/accounts/edit?id=${account.id}`)
                }
                onDelete={
                  transaction.type !== "initial"
                    ? async () => {
                        await deleteItem(transactionsStore, transaction.id);

                        setTransactions((prev) =>
                          prev.filter((t) => t.id !== transaction.id),
                        );

                        setSwipedItem(null);
                      }
                    : undefined
                }
                key={transaction.id}
              >
                <TransactionItem transaction={transaction} account={account} />
              </SwipeableElement>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

و


===== components/AccountCard/index.tsx =====

import { AccountTypes } from "@/utility/shared/accountsTypes";
import { AccountType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";
import { AccountColors } from "@/utility/shared/colors";
import { IconComponent } from "@/icons/types";
import Link from "next/link";

/**
 * Displays an account as a clickable card.
 *
 * The card shows:
 * - Account icon based on its type.
 * - Current account balance.
 * - Account name.
 * - Color theme based on the selected account color.
 *
 * Clicking the card navigates to the account details page.
 *
 * @param props Account information.
 * @param props.id Unique account identifier. Used to generate the details page URL.
 * @param props.name Display name of the account.
 * @param props.color Theme color key used to style the card.
 * @param props.lastBalance Current account balance displayed on the card.
 * @param props.type Account type used to determine the displayed icon.
 *
 * @returns A clickable account card component.
 */

export default function AccountCard({
  id,
  name,
  color,
  lastBalance,
  type,
}: AccountType & { lastBalance: number }) {
  const cardColors = AccountColors[color];
  const CardIcon: IconComponent = AccountTypes[type].icon;

  return (
    <Link href={`/accounts/details?id=${id}`}>
      <div
        className={`w-58 h-36 border p-5 flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-4xl snap-center shrink-0
          ${AccountColors[color].surface}
          ${AccountColors[color].border}`}
        style={{ boxShadow: `0 12px 24px ${cardColors.shadow}` }}
      >
        <div
          className={`h-6.5 w-6.5 grid place-content-center rounded-4xl mr-auto p-0 m-0
            ${AccountColors[color].primary}`}
        >
          <CardIcon height={17} width={17} fill="#fff" />
        </div>
        <div
          className={`flex gap-1.5 mt-auto items-center
            ${AccountColors[color].text}`}
        >
          <h1 className="text-xl font-extrabold">
            {lastBalance.toLocaleString("fa-ir")}
          </h1>
          <span className="text-xs">﷼</span>
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-xs">{name}</h2>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

===== components/AccountsListElement/index.tsx =====

import { AccountType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";
import { AccountColors, PrimaryColors } from "@/utility/shared/colors";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ComponentProps } from "react";
import { AccountBalanceType, BalanceType } from "@/utility/balance/types";
import { AccountTypes } from "@/utility/shared/accountsTypes";

type AccountListElementPropsType = ComponentProps<"a"> & {
  account: AccountType;
  balances: AccountBalanceType[];
  disabled?: boolean;
  desaturated?: boolean;
};

export default function AccountsListElement({
  account,
  balances,
  disabled,
  desaturated,
  ...props
}: AccountListElementPropsType) {
  const balance =
    balances.find((item) => item.id == account.id)?.history?.at(-1)?.balance ??
    0;

  const Icon = AccountTypes[account.type].icon;

  const activeColors = `${AccountColors[account.color].border} ${AccountColors[account.color].surface} ${AccountColors[account.color].text}`;
  const disableColors = `${PrimaryColors.disabled.box}`;

  return (
    <Link
      href={props.href ?? "#"}
      className={`flex items-center justify-between p-4 rounded-2xl border
        ${!disabled ? activeColors : disableColors}
        ${desaturated && "saturate-25 opacity-75"}`}
      {...props}
    >
      <div className="grid gap-2.5">
        <h3 className="text-sm">{account.name}</h3>
        <span className="text-secondary text-xs">
          {balance.toLocaleString("fa-ir")} ﷼
        </span>
      </div>

      <div
        className={`${AccountColors[account.color].primary} rounded-4xl grid place-content-center h-7.5 w-7.5`}
      >
        <Icon height={20} width={20} fill="#fff" />
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

===== components/Button/index.tsx =====

import { PrimaryColors } from "@/utility/shared/colors";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ComponentProps } from "react";

type ButtonParamsType = ComponentProps<"a"> &
  ComponentProps<"button"> & {
    children: React.ReactNode;
    secondary?: boolean;
    red?: boolean;
    disabled?: boolean;
    href?: string;
  };

export default function Button({
  children,
  secondary,
  red,
  disabled,
  href,
  ...props
}: ButtonParamsType) {
  const className = `border text-xs h-11.5 w-full grid place-content-center rounded-2xl transition-all
    ${
      !secondary && !red && !disabled
        ? PrimaryColors.primary.box
        : secondary
          ? PrimaryColors.secondary.box
          : red
            ? PrimaryColors.red.box
            : PrimaryColors.disabled.box
    }
    ${props.className}`;

  if (href && !disabled) {
    return (
      <Link href={href} className="w-full" {...props}>
        <div className={className}>{children}</div>
      </Link>
    );
  }

  return (
    <button type="button" disabled={disabled} className="w-full" {...props}>
      <div className={className}>{children}</div>
    </button>
  );
}

===== components/Capsule/index.tsx =====

"use client";

import { AccountColors, PrimaryColors } from "@/utility/shared/colors";
import { IconComponent } from "@/icons/types";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { ComponentProps } from "react";
import { AccountColorType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";

type CapsulePropsType = ComponentProps<"a"> & {
  Icon: IconComponent;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  href?: string;
  primary?: boolean;
  accountColor?: AccountColorType;
  replace?: boolean;
};

export default function Capsule({
  onClick,
  Icon,
  children,
  href,
  primary,
  accountColor,
  replace,
  ...props
}: CapsulePropsType) {
  const router = useRouter();

  const className = `${props.className ? props.className : primary ? PrimaryColors.primary.box : PrimaryColors.secondary.box} border px-2.5 py-2 rounded-4xl text-xs flex items-center gap-1 text-nowrap`;

  const handleClick = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) => {
    if (onClick) {
      onClick(e);
    }

    if (e.defaultPrevented || !href || href === "#") {
      return;
    }

    if (replace) {
      e.preventDefault();
      router.replace(href);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Link
      {...props}
      href={href ?? "#"}
      className={className}
      onClick={handleClick}
    >
      {children}
      <Icon
        height={15}
        width={15}
        fill={accountColor ? AccountColors[accountColor].primaryValue : ""}
        className={!props.className && primary ? "fill-primary" : ""}
      />
    </Link>
  );
}

===== components/NavbarTitle/index.tsx =====

"use client";

import Right from "@/icons/Right";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { ComponentProps } from "react";

type NavbarTitleType = ComponentProps<"div"> & {
  children?: React.ReactNode;
  title: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
};

export default function NavbarTitle({
  children,
  title,
  disabled,
  onClick,
}: NavbarTitleType) {
  const router = useRouter();

  const handleClick = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (disabled) return;

    if (onClick) {
      onClick(e);
    }

    router.back();
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
      <div className="flex items-cente gap-4">
        <div className="cursor-pointer" onClick={handleClick}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
            {!disabled && (
              <Right height={15} width={15} className="fill-primary" />
            )}
            <h2 className="text-lg font-medium">{title}</h2>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useSortable } from "@dnd-kit/sortable";
import { CSS } from "@dnd-kit/utilities";
import { ReactNode } from "react";

export default function SortableAccountItem({
  account,
  children,
}: {
  account: { id: string };
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  const {
    attributes,
    listeners,
    setNodeRef,
    transform,
    transition,
    isDragging,
  } = useSortable({ id: account.id });

          <circle cx="11" cy="4" r="1.5" />
          <circle cx="11" cy="10" r="1.5" />
          <circle cx="11" cy="16" r="1.5" />
        </svg>
      </button>

      <div className="flex-1">{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== components/SwipeableElement/index.tsx =====

import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from "react";

const ACTION_WIDTH = 100;

export default function SwipeableElement({
  children,
  isOpen,
  onOpenChange,
  onCloseOthers,
  onEdit,
  onDelete,
  defaultAccount,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  onCloseOthers: () => void;
  onEdit?: () => void; 
  onDelete?: () => void;
  defaultAccount?: boolean;
}) {
  const [translateX, setTranslateX] = useState(0);
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);
  const startX = useRef(0);
  const currentX = useRef(0);
  const startTranslateX = useRef(0);

  const canEdit = !!onEdit;
  const canDelete = !!onDelete && !defaultAccount; 

  const minTranslate = canEdit ? -ACTION_WIDTH : 0;
  const maxTranslate = canDelete ? ACTION_WIDTH : 0;

  const isSwipeable = canEdit || canDelete;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen && !isDragging) {
      setTranslateX(0);
    }
  }, [isOpen, isDragging]);

  const handleStart = (clientX: number) => {
    if (!isSwipeable) return;

    if (!isOpen) {
      onCloseOthers();
    }

    startX.current = clientX;
    currentX.current = clientX;
    startTranslateX.current = translateX;
    setIsDragging(true);
  };

  const handleMove = (clientX: number) => {
    if (!isDragging) return;
    currentX.current = clientX;
    const delta = clientX - startX.current;
    const rawTranslate = startTranslateX.current + delta;

    const clampedDelta = Math.max(
      minTranslate,
      Math.min(maxTranslate, rawTranslate),
    );
    setTranslateX(clampedDelta);
  };

  const handleEnd = () => {
    if (!isDragging) return;
    setIsDragging(false);

    const delta = currentX.current - startX.current;
    const threshold = ACTION_WIDTH / 3;

    if (startTranslateX.current === 0) {
      if (delta < -threshold && canEdit) {
        setTranslateX(-ACTION_WIDTH);
        onOpenChange(true);
      } else if (delta > threshold && canDelete) {
        setTranslateX(ACTION_WIDTH);
        onOpenChange(true);
      } else {
        setTranslateX(0);
        onOpenChange(false);
      }
    } else if (startTranslateX.current < 0) {
      if (delta > threshold) {
        setTranslateX(0);
        onOpenChange(false);
      } else {
        setTranslateX(-ACTION_WIDTH);
      }
    } else if (startTranslateX.current > 0) {
      if (delta < -threshold) {
        setTranslateX(0);
        onOpenChange(false);
      } else {
        setTranslateX(ACTION_WIDTH);
      }
    }
  };

  const dragProps = isSwipeable
    ? {
        onMouseDown: (e: React.MouseEvent) => handleStart(e.clientX),
        onMouseMove: (e: React.MouseEvent) =>
          isDragging && handleMove(e.clientX),
        onMouseUp: handleEnd,
        onMouseLeave: () => isDragging && handleEnd(),
        onTouchStart: (e: React.TouchEvent) =>
          handleStart(e.touches[0].clientX),
        onTouchMove: (e: React.TouchEvent) => handleMove(e.touches[0].clientX),
        onTouchEnd: handleEnd,
      }
    : {};

  return (
    <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl">
      {canEdit && (
        <div className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 w-22.5 bg-primary-background border border-primary-border flex items-center justify-center rounded-2xl me-2">
          <button
            onClick={onEdit}
            className="text-primary text-xs font-medium w-full h-full"
          >
            ویرایش
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {canDelete && (
        <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 w-22.5 bg-red-background border border-red-border flex items-center justify-center rounded-2xl ms-2">
          <button
            onClick={onDelete}
            className="text-red text-xs font-medium w-full h-full"
          >
            حذف
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      <div
        {...dragProps}
        className="relative z-10 bg-white select-none rounded-2xl"
        style={{
          transform: `translateX(${translateX}px)`,
          transition: isDragging ? "none" : "transform 0.3s ease-out",
          touchAction: isSwipeable ? "pan-y" : "auto",
          cursor: isSwipeable ? "grab" : "default",
        }}
      >
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== components/TransactionItem/index.tsx =====

"use client";

import { AccountColors } from "@/utility/shared/colors";
import { TransactionType } from "@/utility/transactions/types";
import Wallet from "@/icons/Wallet";
import Link from "next/link";
import { AccountType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { getItem } from "@/utility/db/Database";
import { DatabaseStores } from "@/utility/keys/database";

type TransactionItemParamsType = {
  transaction: TransactionType;
  account: AccountType;
};

export default function TransactionItem({
  transaction,
  account,
}: TransactionItemParamsType) {
  const { id: txID, name, time, type, value, debit, credit } = transaction;
  const { accountsStore } = DatabaseStores;

  const [transferNames, setTransferNames] = useState<string[]>(["", ""]);

  const txName =
    type === "initial"
      ? "موجودی اولیه"
      : type === "transfer"
        ? transferNames[0] + " › " + transferNames[1]
        : name;

  const date = new Date(time);
  const isSameYear =
    new Date().toLocaleDateString("fa-ir", { year: "numeric" }) ===
    date.toLocaleDateString("fa-ir", { year: "numeric" });
  const dateOptions: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions = {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "long",
    year: !isSameYear ? "numeric" : undefined,
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      if (type !== "transfer") return;

      const [creditAcc, debitAcc] = (await Promise.all([
        getItem(accountsStore, credit),
        getItem(accountsStore, debit),
      ])) as (AccountType | undefined)[];

      setTransferNames([creditAcc?.name ?? "", debitAcc?.name ?? ""]);
    })();
  }, [type, debit, credit]);

  return (
    <Link
      href={`/transactions/detail?id=${txID}`}
      className="w-full flex items-center gap-2.5"
    >
      <div
        className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl grid place-content-center shrink-0 ${AccountColors[account.color ?? "black"].primary}`}
      >
        <Wallet fill="#fff" height={25} width={25} />
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 w-full">
        <h1 className="text-xs">{txName}</h1>
        <p className="text-xs text-secondary">
          {date.toLocaleDateString("fa-ir", dateOptions)}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div
        className={`text-xs flex items-center gap-1 font-medium
          ${type === "debit" ? "text-income-primary" : type === "credit" ? "text-expense-primary" : ""}`}
      >
        {Number(value).toLocaleString("fa-ir")}
        <span
          className="grid place-content-center"
          style={{
            color:
              type === "initial" || type === "transfer"
                ? "transparent"
                : "inherit",
          }}
        >
          {type === "debit" ? "+" : "-"}
        </span>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

};

export default config;

===== utility/accounts/types.ts =====

import { AccountColors } from "../shared/colors";
import { AccountTypes } from "../shared/accountsTypes";

/**
 * Defines `Account Colors` type by keys of {@link AccountColors} element
 *
 * Every account haves one of these colors
 */

export type AccountColorType = keyof typeof AccountColors;

/**
 * Defines `Account Types` type by keys of {@link AccountTypes} element
 *
 * Every account is one of these types
 */

export type AccountTypesType = keyof typeof AccountTypes;

/**
 * Defines Account Object type
 *
 * This object contains:
 * @param id Account ID, generated automatically when the account created
 * @param name Account name
 * @param color Account color key
 * @param lastBalance Account last balance, calculated through methods
 * @param type Account type
 */

export type AccountType = {
  id: string;
  order: number;
  name: string;
  color: AccountColorType;
  type: AccountTypesType;
  default?: boolean;
  hidden?: boolean;
};

===== utility/backup-restore/methods.ts =====

import { AccountType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";
import { TransactionType } from "@/utility/transactions/types";
import { TagType } from "@/utility/shared/tagTypes";

export type BackupType = {
  time: string;
  accounts: AccountType[];
  transactions: TransactionType[];
  tags: TagType[];
};

export async function getBackup(
  accounts: AccountType[],
  transactions: TransactionType[],
  tags: TagType[],
) {
  const backupData: BackupType = {
    time: new Date().toISOString(),
    accounts,
    transactions,
    tags,
  };

  const dataStr = JSON.stringify(backupData, null, 2);
  const blob = new Blob([dataStr], { type: "application/json" });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement("a");
  a.href = url;
  a.download = `pocket-backup-${new Date().toLocaleDateString("fa-IR")}.pbak`;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  document.body.removeChild(a);
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

===== utility/balance/service/balancesStore.ts =====

"use client";

import { AccountBalanceType } from "../types";

class BalancesStore {
  private balances: AccountBalanceType[] = [];
  private listeners: Set<() => void> = new Set();

  private notify() {
    this.listeners.forEach((listener) => listener());
  }

  getBalances(): AccountBalanceType[] {
    return [...this.balances];
  }

  setBalances(data: AccountBalanceType[]) {
    this.balances = [...data];
    this.notify();
  }

  addBalance(balance: AccountBalanceType) {
    this.balances.push(balance);
    this.notify();
  }

  updateBalance(updatedBalance: AccountBalanceType) {
    const index = this.balances.findIndex((b) => b.id === updatedBalance.id);
    if (index !== -1) {
      this.balances[index] = { ...updatedBalance };
      this.notify();
    } else {
      this.addBalance(updatedBalance);
    }
  }

  removeBalance(id: string) {
    this.balances = this.balances.filter((b) => b.id !== id);
    this.notify();
  }

  subscribe = (listener: () => void) => {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener);
  };

  getSnapshot = () => this.balances;

  clear() {
    this.balances = [];
    this.notify();
  }
}

export const balancesStore = new BalancesStore();
export default balancesStore;

===== utility/balance/service/useBalances.ts =====

"use client";

import { useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
import { balancesStore } from "./balancesStore";

export default function useBalances() {
  const balances = useSyncExternalStore(
    balancesStore.subscribe,
    balancesStore.getSnapshot,
    balancesStore.getSnapshot,
  );

  return {
    balances,
    addBalance: balancesStore.addBalance.bind(balancesStore),
    updateBalance: balancesStore.updateBalance.bind(balancesStore),
    removeBalance: balancesStore.removeBalance.bind(balancesStore),
    clear: balancesStore.clear.bind(balancesStore),
    getBalances: balancesStore.getBalances.bind(balancesStore),
    getSnapshot: balancesStore.getSnapshot.bind(balancesStore),
    setBalances: balancesStore.setBalances.bind(balancesStore),
    subscribe: balancesStore.subscribe.bind(balancesStore),
  };
}

===== utility/balance/types.ts =====

/**
 * Represents the balance of an account at a specific point in time.
 *
 * This type is mainly used for generating balance history charts or
 * displaying historical account balances after each transaction.
 */
export type BalanceType = {
  /**
   * Unix timestamp (milliseconds) representing when the balance was recorded.
   */
  time: number;

  /**
   * Account balance immediately after the transaction at `time`.
   */
  balance: number;
};

/**
 * Represents the complete balance history for multiple accounts.
 *
 * Each item corresponds to a single account and contains its identifier
 * alongside an optional chronological balance history.
 *
 * @example
 * [
 *   {
 *     id: 1,
 *     history: [
 *       { time: 1710000000000, balance: 100000 },
 *       { time: 1710003600000, balance: 85000 }
 *     ]
 *   },
 *   {
 *     id: 2,
 *     history: []
 *   }
 * ]
 */
export type AccountBalanceType = {
  /**
   * Unique identifier of the account.
   */
  id: string;

  /**
   * Chronological balance history of the account.
   *
   * If omitted or empty, the account is considered to have no recorded
   * balance history.
   */
  history?: BalanceType[];
};

===== utility/db/Database.ts =====

const NUMERIC_FIELDS: Record<string, string[]> = {
  accounts: ["order"],
  transactions: ["time", "value"],
  tags: ["order"],
};

function castRow<T>(store: string, row: any): T {
  if (!row) return row;
  const fields = NUMERIC_FIELDS[store] || [];
  for (let key in row) {
    if (row[key] === null) {
      row[key] = fields.includes(key) ? 0 : "";
    } else if (fields.includes(key)) {
      row[key] = Number(row[key]);
    }
  }
  return row as T;
}

async function handleUnauthorized(res: Response) {
  if (res.status === 401) {
    window.location.href = "/splash";
    throw new Error("Unauthorized");
  }
}

export async function getAll<T>(store: string): Promise<T[]> {
  const res = await fetch(`/api/${store}`);
  await handleUnauthorized(res);
  if (!res.ok) {
    const errorData = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(errorData.error || `خطا در دریافت اطلاعات ${store}`);
  }
  const data = await res.json();
  if (!Array.isArray(data)) return [];
  return data.map((row: any) => castRow<T>(store, row));
}

export async function getItem<T>(
  store: string,
  id: string,
): Promise<T | undefined> {
  const res = await fetch(`/api/${store}/${id}`);
  await handleUnauthorized(res);
  if (!res.ok) return undefined;
  const data = await res.json();
  if (!data) return undefined;
  return castRow<T>(store, data);
}

export async function putItem<T extends { id: string } | { id: string }[]>(
  store: string,
  item: T,
): Promise<void> {
  const res = await fetch(`/api/${store}`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify(item),
  });
  await handleUnauthorized(res);
  if (!res.ok) {
    const errorData = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(errorData.error || "خطا در ذخیره‌سازی اطلاعات");
  }
}

export async function deleteItem(store: string, id: string): Promise<void> {
  const res = await fetch(`/api/${store}/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
  await handleUnauthorized(res);
  if (!res.ok) {
    const errorData = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(errorData.error || "خطا در حذف اطلاعات");
  }
}

export async function clearStore(store: string): Promise<void> {
  const res = await fetch(`/api/${store}`, { method: "DELETE" });
  await handleUnauthorized(res);
  if (!res.ok) {
    const errorData = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(errorData.error || "خطا در پاکسازی اطلاعات");
  }
}

import { AccountTypes } from "@/utility/shared/accountsTypes";

    ? s.toString().replace(/[۰-۹]/g, (d) => "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹".indexOf(d).toString())

export const AMOUNT_NUMBER_FORMAT = '_(* #,##0_);_(* (#,##0);_(* "-"_);_(@_)';
