===== utility/import/generateTemplate.ts =====

import ExcelJS from "exceljs";
import { AccountType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";
import { TagType, TagItemTypes } from "@/utility/shared/tagTypes";
import { TransactionCategories } from "@/utility/shared/transactionCategories";
import { AccountTypes } from "@/utility/shared/accountsTypes";
import {
  AMOUNT_NUMBER_FORMAT,
  excelColorOptions,
  excelTypeOptions,
  excelTagTypeOptions,
} from "./excelConstants";

export async function generateExcelTemplate(
  existingAccounts: AccountType[],
  existingTags: TagType[],
) {
  const wb = new ExcelJS.Workbook();

  const centerAlign = {
    horizontal: "center",
    vertical: "middle",
    readingOrder: "rtl",
  } as const;
  const rightAlign = {
    horizontal: "right",
    vertical: "middle",
    readingOrder: "rtl",
  } as const;

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------
  // شیت ۱: حساب‌ها و برچسب‌ها
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------
  const ws1 = wb.addWorksheet("حساب‌ها و برچسب‌ها", {
    views: [{ rightToLeft: true }],
  });

  ws1.columns = [
    { width: 10 },
    { width: 25 },
    { width: 15 },
    { width: 15 },
    { width: 15 },
    { width: 15 },
    { width: 15 },
    { width: 10 },
    { width: 25 },
    { width: 15 },
    { width: 15 },
    { width: 15 },
    { width: 2 },
    { width: 40 },
  ];

  const guideLinesSheet1 = [
    "راهنما:",
    "۱) در ستون 'ردیف'، ترتیب نمایش حساب‌ها و برچسب‌ها را وارد کنید (دوبار وارد کردن یک عدد مجاز نیست).",
    "۲) حساب‌ها و برچسب‌های جدید را فقط با نام در ستون‌های مربوطه وارد کنید.",
    "۳) کدها به صورت خودکار توسط برنامه اختصاص می‌یابند، نیازی به وارد کردن کد نیست.",
    "۴) برای حساب‌ها، نوع و رنگ را از لیست کشویی انتخاب کنید. اگر خالی بگذارید، پیش‌فرض (بانکی/مشکی) ثبت می‌شود.",
    "۵) نوع و رنگ حساب‌های پیش‌فرض سیستم قابل تغییر نیست.",
    "۶) برای دسته‌بندی‌های ثابت برنامه از شیت دوم استفاده کنید و نیازی به ساخت برچسب نیست.",
  ];

  ws1.getCell("A1").value = "ردیف";
  ws1.getCell("B1").value = "نام حساب";
  ws1.getCell("C1").value = "کد حساب";
  ws1.getCell("D1").value = "نوع حساب";
  ws1.getCell("E1").value = "کد نوع";
  ws1.getCell("F1").value = "رنگ حساب";
  ws1.getCell("G1").value = "کد رنگ";
  ws1.getCell("H1").value = "ردیف";
  ws1.getCell("I1").value = "نام برچسب";
  ws1.getCell("J1").value = "کد برچسب";
  ws1.getCell("K1").value = "نوع برچسب";
  ws1.getCell("L1").value = "کد نوع برچسب";
  ws1.getCell("N1").value = guideLinesSheet1[0];

  const accountsForExcel = existingAccounts.filter(
    (a) => a.name !== "وجوه در راه",
  );

  const maxRows1 = 500;
  for (let i = 0; i < maxRows1; i++) {
    const r = i + 2;
    const acc = accountsForExcel[i];
    const tag = existingTags[i];

    if (acc) {
      ws1.getCell(`A${r}`).value = acc.order ?? i + 1;
      ws1.getCell(`B${r}`).value = acc.name;
      ws1.getCell(`C${r}`).value = acc.id;

      if (acc.default) {
        ws1.getCell(`D${r}`).value = "پیش‌فرض";
        ws1.getCell(`F${r}`).value = "پیش‌فرض";
        ws1.getCell(`D${r}`).fill = {
          type: "pattern",
          pattern: "solid",
          fgColor: { argb: "FFF0F0F0" },
        };
        ws1.getCell(`F${r}`).fill = {
          type: "pattern",
          pattern: "solid",
          fgColor: { argb: "FFF0F0F0" },
        };
      } else {
        ws1.getCell(`D${r}`).value =
          (AccountTypes as any)[acc.type]?.name || "حساب بانکی";
        ws1.getCell(`F${r}`).value =
          excelColorOptions.find((c) => c.key === acc.color)?.label || "مشکی";
      }
    }
    if (tag) {
      ws1.getCell(`H${r}`).value = tag.order ?? i + 1;
      ws1.getCell(`I${r}`).value = tag.name;
      ws1.getCell(`J${r}`).value = tag.id;
      ws1.getCell(`K${r}`).value =
        TagItemTypes.find((t) => t.value === tag.type)?.label || "سایر";
    }

    if (guideLinesSheet1[i + 1])
      ws1.getCell(`N${r}`).value = guideLinesSheet1[i + 1];

    for (let c = 1; c <= 14; c++)
      ws1.getCell(r, c).alignment = c === 14 ? rightAlign : centerAlign;
  }

  ws1.getColumn(3).hidden = true;
  ws1.getColumn(5).hidden = true;
  ws1.getColumn(7).hidden = true;
  ws1.getColumn(10).hidden = true;
  ws1.getColumn(12).hidden = true;

  for (let r = 2; r <= maxRows1 + 1; r++) {
    ws1.getCell(`A${r}`).dataValidation = {
      type: "custom",
      allowBlank: true,
      formulae: [`COUNTIF($A$2:$A$501,A${r})<=1`],
      showErrorMessage: true,
      errorTitle: "نامعتبر",
      error: "ردیف تکراری مجاز نیست",
    };
    ws1.getCell(`H${r}`).dataValidation = {
      type: "custom",
      allowBlank: true,
      formulae: [`COUNTIF($H$2:$H$501,H${r})<=1`],
      showErrorMessage: true,
      errorTitle: "نامعتبر",
      error: "ردیف تکراری مجاز نیست",
    };

    if (ws1.getCell(`D${r}`).value !== "پیش‌فرض") {
      ws1.getCell(`D${r}`).dataValidation = {
        type: "list",
        allowBlank: true,
        formulae: [`"${excelTypeOptions.join(",")}"`],
      };
      ws1.getCell(`F${r}`).dataValidation = {
        type: "list",
        allowBlank: true,
        formulae: [`"${excelColorOptions.map((c) => c.label).join(",")}"`],
      };
    }

    ws1.getCell(`K${r}`).dataValidation = {
      type: "list",
      allowBlank: true,
      formulae: [`"${excelTagTypeOptions.join(",")}"`],
    };
  }

  excelColorOptions.forEach((opt) => {
    ws1.addConditionalFormatting({
      ref: `B2:B${maxRows1 + 1}`,
      rules: [
        {
          type: "expression",
          formulae: [`EXACT($F2,"${opt.label}")`],
          priority: 1,
          style: {
            fill: {
              type: "pattern",
              pattern: "solid",
              bgColor: { argb: opt.argb },
            },
            font: { color: { argb: "FFFFFFFF" }, bold: true },
          },
        },
      ],
    });
  });

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------
  // شیت ۲: دسته‌بندی‌ها
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------
  const ws2 = wb.addWorksheet("دسته‌بندی‌ها", {
    views: [{ rightToLeft: true }],
  });
  ws2.getRow(1).height = 30;

  ws2.mergeCells("A1:E1");
  ws2.getCell("A1").value = "دسته‌بندی‌های هزینه";
  ws2.mergeCells("G1:K1");
  ws2.getCell("G1").value = "دسته‌بندی‌های درآمد";
  ws2.getRow(1).eachCell((cell) => {
    cell.alignment = centerAlign;
    cell.font = { bold: true };
  });

  const placeCat = (group: any, startRow: number, startCol: number) => {
    ws2.mergeCells(startRow, startCol, startRow, startCol + 1);
    ws2.getCell(startRow, startCol).value = group.name;
    ws2.getCell(startRow, startCol).alignment = centerAlign;
    ws2.getCell(startRow, startCol).font = { bold: true };

    ws2.getCell(startRow + 1, startCol).value = "کد";
    ws2.getCell(startRow + 1, startCol + 1).value = "نام دسته‌بندی";
    ws2.getCell(startRow + 1, startCol).alignment = centerAlign;
    ws2.getCell(startRow + 1, startCol + 1).alignment = centerAlign;

    group.sub.forEach((sub: any, sIdx: number) => {
      const r = startRow + 2 + sIdx;
      ws2.getCell(r, startCol).value = sub.id;
      ws2.getCell(r, startCol + 1).value = sub.name;
      ws2.getCell(r, startCol).alignment = centerAlign;
      ws2.getCell(r, startCol + 1).alignment = centerAlign;
    });
    return startRow + 2 + group.sub.length + 1;
  };

  let rowPtrExp = [2, 2];
  TransactionCategories.expense.forEach((group, idx) => {
    rowPtrExp[idx % 2] = placeCat(
      group,
      rowPtrExp[idx % 2],
      idx % 2 === 0 ? 1 : 4,
    );
  });

  let rowPtrInc = [2, 2];
  TransactionCategories.income.forEach((group, idx) => {
    rowPtrInc[idx % 2] = placeCat(
      group,
      rowPtrInc[idx % 2],
      idx % 2 === 0 ? 7 : 10,
    );
  });

  let lRow = 2,
    nRow = 2;
  TransactionCategories.income.forEach((g) =>
    g.sub.forEach((s) => {
      ws2.getCell(`L${lRow}`).value = s.id;
      ws2.getCell(`M${lRow}`).value = s.name;
      lRow++;
    }),
  );
  TransactionCategories.expense.forEach((g) =>
    g.sub.forEach((s) => {
      ws2.getCell(`N${nRow}`).value = s.id;
      ws2.getCell(`O${nRow}`).value = s.name;
      nRow++;
    }),
  );

  ws2.getColumn(12).hidden = true;
  ws2.getColumn(13).hidden = true;
  ws2.getColumn(14).hidden = true;
  ws2.getColumn(15).hidden = true;

  const colWidths = [10, 10, 10, 10, 10, 10, 10, 10, 10, 10, 10];
  ws2.eachRow({ includeEmpty: true }, (row) => {
    row.eachCell({ includeEmpty: true }, (cell, colNumber) => {
      if (cell.value && colNumber <= 11) {
        const len = cell.value.toString().length;
        if (len > colWidths[colNumber - 1]) colWidths[colNumber - 1] = len;
      }
    });
  });
  for (let i = 0; i < 11; i++) {
    let width = colWidths[i] + 2;
    if (i < 5) width = Math.round(width * 0.75);
    ws2.getColumn(i + 1).width = width;
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------
  // شیت ۳: تراکنش‌ها
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------
  const ws3 = wb.addWorksheet("تراکنش‌ها", { views: [{ rightToLeft: true }] });

  ws3.columns = [
    { width: 12 },
    { width: 8 },
    { width: 12 },
    { width: 25 },
    { width: 12 },
    { width: 25 },
    { width: 12 },
    { width: 15 },
    { width: 15 },
    { width: 25 },
    { width: 30 },
    { width: 15 },
    { width: 2 },
    { width: 20 },
  ];

  const guideLinesSheet3 = [
    "راهنما:",
    "۱) نام حساب و نام برچسب را از لیست کشویی انتخاب کنید (کد آن‌ها خودکار وارد می‌شود).",
    "۲) نوع تراکنش را انتخاب کنید.",
    "۳) برای دریافت/پرداخت: کد دسته‌بندی را در ستون 'کد دسته‌بندی' وارد کنید. نام آن خودکار در ستون بعدی نمایش داده می‌شود.",
    "۴) برای انتقال: نام حساب مقصد را در ستون 'کد دسته‌بندی' وارد کنید. در ستون نام، علامت ✓ نشان‌دهنده صحت نام مقصد است.",
    "۵) تاریخ را با فرمت 1405/01/01 و ساعت را با فرمت 12:00 وارد کنید.",
    "۶) در ستون مبلغ فقط رقم تراکنش را وارد کنید. ستون یادداشت اختیاری است.",
  ];

  ws3.getRow(1).height = 30;
  ws3.mergeCells("A1:L1");
  ws3.getCell("A1").value = "تراکنش‌ها";
  ws3.getCell("A1").alignment = centerAlign;
  ws3.getCell("A1").font = { bold: true, size: 14 };

  ws3.getCell("A2").value = "تاریخ";
  ws3.getCell("B2").value = "ساعت";
  ws3.getCell("C2").value = "کد حساب";
  ws3.getCell("D2").value = "نام حساب";
  ws3.getCell("E2").value = "کد برچسب";
  ws3.getCell("F2").value = "نام برچسب";
  ws3.getCell("G2").value = "کد نوع";
  ws3.getCell("H2").value = "نوع تراکنش";
  ws3.getCell("I2").value = "کد دسته‌بندی / مقصد";
  ws3.getCell("J2").value = "نام دسته‌بندی / تایید";
  ws3.getCell("K2").value = "یادداشت";
  ws3.getCell("L2").value = "مبلغ";
  ws3.getCell("N2").value = "محدوده برچسب";
  ws3.getCell("O2").value = guideLinesSheet3[0];

  for (let c = 1; c <= 15; c++) {
    ws3.getCell(2, c).alignment = c === 15 ? rightAlign : centerAlign;
    ws3.getCell(2, c).font = { bold: true };
  }

  ws3.getColumn(3).hidden = true;
  ws3.getColumn(5).hidden = true;
  ws3.getColumn(7).hidden = true;
  ws3.getColumn(14).hidden = true;

  const PREFORMAT_ROWS = 3000;
  for (let r = 3; r <= PREFORMAT_ROWS; r++) {
    ws3.getCell(`A${r}`).numFmt = "@";
    ws3.getCell(`B${r}`).numFmt = "@";
    ws3.getCell(`L${r}`).numFmt = AMOUNT_NUMBER_FORMAT;

    if (r - 2 < guideLinesSheet3.length)
      ws3.getCell(`O${r}`).value = guideLinesSheet3[r - 2];

    for (let c = 1; c <= 15; c++) {
      ws3.getCell(r, c).alignment = c === 15 ? rightAlign : centerAlign;
    }

    ws3.getCell(`C${r}`).value = {
      formula: `IFERROR(VLOOKUP(D${r},'حساب‌ها و برچسب‌ها'!$B$2:$C$501,2,FALSE),"")`,
    };
    ws3.getCell(`G${r}`).value = {
      formula: `IF(H${r}="دریافت","debit",IF(H${r}="پرداخت","credit",IF(H${r}="انتقال","transfer",IF(H${r}="افتتاح","initial",""))))`,
    };
    ws3.getCell(`E${r}`).value = {
      formula: `IF(G${r}="initial",0,IFERROR(VLOOKUP(F${r},'حساب‌ها و برچسب‌ها'!$I$2:$J$501,2,FALSE),0))`,
    };
    ws3.getCell(`N${r}`).value = {
      formula: `IF(G${r}="initial","'حساب‌ها و برچسب‌ها'!$Z$1","'حساب‌ها و برچسب‌ها'!$I$2:$I$501")`,
    };
    ws3.getCell(`J${r}`).value = {
      formula: `IF(I${r}="","",IF(G${r}="transfer",IF(ISNUMBER(MATCH(I${r},'حساب‌ها و برچسب‌ها'!$B$2:$B$501,0)),"✓","✗"),IF(G${r}="credit",IFERROR(VLOOKUP(I${r},'دسته‌بندی‌ها'!$N$2:$O$501,2,FALSE),"✗"),IFERROR(VLOOKUP(I${r},'دسته‌بندی‌ها'!$L$2:$M$501,2,FALSE),"✗"))))`,
    };

    ws3.getCell(`D${r}`).dataValidation = {
      type: "list",
      allowBlank: true,
      formulae: ["'حساب‌ها و برچسب‌ها'!$B$2:$B$501"],
      showErrorMessage: true,
      errorTitle: "نامعتبر",
      error: "از لیست انتخاب کنید",
    };
    ws3.getCell(`F${r}`).dataValidation = {
      type: "list",
      allowBlank: true,
      formulae: [`INDIRECT(N${r})`],
      showErrorMessage: true,
      errorTitle: "نامعتبر",
      error: "برای این نوع تراکنش برچسب مجاز نیست",
    };
    ws3.getCell(`H${r}`).dataValidation = {
      type: "list",
      allowBlank: true,
      formulae: ['"دریافت,پرداخت,انتقال,افتتاح"'],
      showErrorMessage: true,
      errorTitle: "نامعتبر",
      error: "مقدار مجاز را انتخاب کنید",
    };
  }

  const buffer = await wb.xlsx.writeBuffer();
  const blob = new Blob([buffer], {
    type: "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
  });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement("a");
  a.href = url;
  a.download = "نمونه-ایمپورت-تراکنش‌ها.xlsx";
  a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

و


===== utility/import/importToDatabase.ts =====

import { DatabaseStores } from "@/utility/keys/database";
import { putItem } from "@/utility/db/Database";
import { AccountType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";
import { TagType } from "@/utility/shared/tagTypes";
import { TransactionCategories } from "@/utility/shared/transactionCategories";
import { jalaliToTimestamp } from "@/utility/shared/timeMethods";
import { MessageType } from "@/app/message/page";
import { PreviewData } from "./excelConstants";

const chunkArray = (array: any[], size: number) => {
  const result = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < array.length; i += size) {
    result.push(array.slice(i, i + size));
  }
  return result;
};

export async function importDataToDatabase(
  previewData: PreviewData,
  existingAccounts: AccountType[],
  existingTags: TagType[],
  router: any,
) {
  const validCategoryIds = new Set<any>();
  TransactionCategories.expense.forEach((g) =>
    g.sub.forEach((s) => validCategoryIds.add(s.id)),
  );
  TransactionCategories.income.forEach((g) =>
    g.sub.forEach((s) => validCategoryIds.add(s.id)),
  );

  const errors: string[] = [];
  const validTypes = ["debit", "credit", "transfer", "initial"];

  const validTxs = previewData.rawTransactions.filter((tx) => {
    const hasDate = tx.date && String(tx.date).trim() !== "";
    const hasAcc = tx.accountName && String(tx.accountName).trim() !== "";
    return hasDate || hasAcc;
  });

  for (const tx of validTxs) {
    const missing: string[] = [];

    if (!tx.date) missing.push("تاریخ");
    if (!tx.time && tx.type !== "initial") missing.push("ساعت");
    if (!tx.accountName) missing.push("نام حساب");
    if (!tx.type || !validTypes.includes(tx.type)) missing.push("نوع تراکنش");

    if (
      tx.type !== "initial" &&
      (tx.catOrDestCode === undefined || tx.catOrDestCode === "")
    ) {
      missing.push("کد دسته‌بندی / مقصد");
    }

    if (
      tx.value === undefined ||
      tx.value === null ||
      tx.value < 0 ||
      (tx.value === 0 && tx.type !== "initial")
    ) {
      missing.push("مبلغ");
    }

    if (missing.length > 0) {
      errors.push(
        `در ردیف ${tx.excelRow} فایل اکسل، مشخصه‌ی ${missing.join("، ")} به درستی وارد نشده‌است.`,
      );
      continue;
    }

    if (tx.type === "transfer") {
      const destName = String(tx.catOrDestCode);
      const existsInUploaded = previewData.accounts.some(
        (a) => a.name === destName,
      );
      if (!existsInUploaded)
        errors.push(
          `در ردیف ${tx.excelRow} از حساب مقصد نامعتبری استفاده شده است.`,
        );
    } else if (tx.type === "credit" || tx.type === "debit") {
      if (!validCategoryIds.has(tx.catOrDestCode))
        errors.push(
          `در ردیف ${tx.excelRow} از دسته‌بندی نامعتبری استفاده شده است.`,
        );
    }
  }

  if (errors.length > 0) {
    const message: MessageType = {
      title: "خطا در ورود اطلاعات",
      type: "error",
      message: errors,
    };
    sessionStorage.setItem("message", JSON.stringify(message));
    router.replace("/message");
    return;
  }

  try {
    const accIdResolver = new Map<string, string>();
    const tagIdResolver = new Map<string, string>();

    existingAccounts.forEach((a) => {
      accIdResolver.set(a.name, a.id);
      accIdResolver.set(a.id, a.id);
    });
    existingTags.forEach((t) => {
      tagIdResolver.set(t.name, t.id);
      tagIdResolver.set(String(t.id), t.id);
    });

    const accCreateDateMap = new Map<string, number>();
    validTxs.forEach((tx) => {
      const [y, m, d] = String(tx.date).split("/").map(Number);
      const timeStr =
        tx.type === "initial" ? "00:00:00" : String(tx.time || "00:00");
      let h = 0,
        min = 0,
        s = 0;
      if (timeStr.includes(":")) {
        const parts = timeStr.split(":").map(Number);
        h = parts[0] || 0;
        min = parts[1] || 0;
        s = parts[2] || 0;
      } else if (!isNaN(Number(timeStr))) {
        const fraction = Number(timeStr);
        if (fraction > 0 && fraction < 1) {
          const totalSeconds = Math.round(fraction * 24 * 3600);
          h = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 3600);
          min = Math.floor((totalSeconds % 3600) / 60);
          s = totalSeconds % 60;
        }
      }
      const time =
        jalaliToTimestamp([y, m, d]) + h * 3600000 + min * 60000 + s * 1000;

      const accName = tx.accountName;
      if (
        accName &&
        (!accCreateDateMap.has(accName) ||
          time < accCreateDateMap.get(accName)!)
      ) {
        accCreateDateMap.set(accName, time);
      }

      if (tx.type === "transfer") {
        const destName = String(tx.catOrDestCode);
        if (
          destName &&
          (!accCreateDateMap.has(destName) ||
            time < accCreateDateMap.get(destName)!)
        ) {
          accCreateDateMap.set(destName, time);
        }
      }
    });

    const accountsToSave: AccountType[] = [];
    let newAccountsCount = 0;
    let updatedAccountsCount = 0;

    for (const acc of previewData.accounts) {
      if (acc.isNew && !accIdResolver.has(acc.name)) {
        const newId = crypto.randomUUID();
        const newAcc: AccountType = {
          id: newId,
          name: acc.name,
          type: acc.type as any,
          color: acc.color as any,
          default: false,
          order: acc.order,
        };
        accountsToSave.push(newAcc);
        accIdResolver.set(acc.name, newId);
        newAccountsCount++;
      } else if (acc.needsUpdate && acc.id) {
        const existingAcc = existingAccounts.find((a) => a.id === acc.id);
        if (existingAcc) {
          accountsToSave.push({
            ...existingAcc,
            type: acc.type as any,
            color: acc.color as any,
            order: existingAcc.name === "وجوه در راه" ? 999 : acc.order,
          });
          updatedAccountsCount++;
        }
      }
    }
    for (const chunk of chunkArray(accountsToSave, 50)) {
      await putItem(DatabaseStores.accountsStore, chunk);
    }

    const tagsToSave: TagType[] = [];
    let newTagsCount = 0;
    let updatedTagsCount = 0;

    for (const tag of previewData.tags) {
      if (tag.isNew && !tagIdResolver.has(tag.name)) {
        const newId = crypto.randomUUID();
        tagsToSave.push({
          id: newId,
          name: tag.name,
          type: tag.type as any,
          order: tag.order,
        });
        tagIdResolver.set(tag.name, newId);
        newTagsCount++;
      } else if (tag.needsUpdate && tag.id) {
        const existingTag = existingTags.find((t) => t.id === tag.id);
        if (existingTag) {
          tagsToSave.push({
            ...existingTag,
            type: tag.type as any,
            order: tag.order,
          });
          updatedTagsCount++;
        }
      }
    }
    for (const chunk of chunkArray(tagsToSave, 50)) {
      await putItem(DatabaseStores.tagsStore, chunk);
    }

    const txsToSave: any[] = [];
    const accountsWithInitial = new Set<string>();

    for (const tx of validTxs) {
      const [y, m, d] = String(tx.date).split("/").map(Number);
      const timeStr =
        tx.type === "initial" ? "00:00:00" : String(tx.time || "00:00");
      let h = 0,
        min = 0,
        s = 0;
      if (timeStr.includes(":")) {
        const parts = timeStr.split(":").map(Number);
        h = parts[0] || 0;
        min = parts[1] || 0;
        s = parts[2] || 0;
      } else if (!isNaN(Number(timeStr))) {
        const fraction = Number(timeStr);
        if (fraction > 0 && fraction < 1) {
          const totalSeconds = Math.round(fraction * 24 * 3600);
          h = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 3600);
          min = Math.floor((totalSeconds % 3600) / 60);
          s = totalSeconds % 60;
        }
      }
      const time =
        jalaliToTimestamp([y, m, d]) + h * 3600000 + min * 60000 + s * 1000;

      let debitId = "",
        creditId = "",
        tagId = "",
        catId = "";
      const accId = accIdResolver.get(tx.accountName) || "";
      const tId = tagIdResolver.get(tx.tagName) || "";

      if (tx.type !== "transfer" && tx.type !== "initial")
        catId = tx.catOrDestCode || 0;

      if (tx.type === "debit" || tx.type === "initial") {
        debitId = accId;
        creditId = catId;
      } else if (tx.type === "credit") {
        creditId = accId;
        debitId = catId;
      } else if (tx.type === "transfer") {
        creditId = accId;
        debitId = accIdResolver.get(String(tx.catOrDestCode)) || "";
      }
      tagId = tId;

      txsToSave.push({
        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
        time,
        name: tx.note || "",
        type: tx.type,
        debit: debitId,
        credit: creditId,
        value: tx.value,
        note: tx.note || "",
        tag: tagId,
      });

      if (tx.type === "initial" && debitId) accountsWithInitial.add(debitId);
    }

    for (const acc of previewData.accounts) {
      if (acc.isNew) {
        const newAccId = accIdResolver.get(acc.name);
        if (newAccId && !accountsWithInitial.has(newAccId)) {
          const firstTxTime = accCreateDateMap.get(acc.name) || Date.now();
          const initialTime = firstTxTime - 60000;
          txsToSave.push({
            id: crypto.randomUUID(),
            time: initialTime,
            name: "موجودی اولیه",
            type: "initial",
            debit: newAccId,
            credit: 0,
            value: 0,
            note: "",
            tag: 0,
          });
        }
      }
    }

    for (const chunk of chunkArray(txsToSave, 50)) {
      await putItem(DatabaseStores.transactionsStore, chunk);
    }

    const txCount = validTxs.length;
    const successMessage: MessageType = {
      title: "عملیات با موفقیت انجام شد",
      type: "done",
      message: [
        `${newAccountsCount.toLocaleString("fa-IR")} حساب جدید ایجاد شد`,
        `${updatedAccountsCount.toLocaleString("fa-IR")} حساب بروزرسانی شد`,
        `${newTagsCount.toLocaleString("fa-IR")} برچسب جدید ایجاد شد`,
        `${updatedTagsCount.toLocaleString("fa-IR")} برچسب بروزرسانی شد`,
        `با استفاده از موارد فوق، ${txCount.toLocaleString("fa-IR")} تراکنش افزوده شد`,
      ],
    };

    sessionStorage.setItem("message", JSON.stringify(successMessage));
    router.replace("/message");
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Import error:", error);
    const errorMessage: MessageType = {
      title: "خطای سیستم",
      type: "error",
      message: [
        "خطایی هنگام ثبت اطلاعات در دیتابیس رخ داده است.",
        error.message || "",
      ],
    };
    sessionStorage.setItem("message", JSON.stringify(errorMessage));
    router.replace("/message");
  }
}

و


===== utility/import/parseExcelFile.ts =====

import ExcelJS from "exceljs";
import { AccountType, AccountTypesType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";
import { TagType, TagItemTypes } from "@/utility/shared/tagTypes";
import { AccountTypes } from "@/utility/shared/accountsTypes";
import { faToEn, excelColorOptions, PreviewData } from "./excelConstants";

export async function parseUploadedExcel(
  buffer: ArrayBuffer,
  existingAccounts: AccountType[],
  existingTags: TagType[],
): Promise<PreviewData> {
  const wb = new ExcelJS.Workbook();
  await wb.xlsx.load(buffer);

  const ws1 = wb.getWorksheet(1);
  const ws3 = wb.getWorksheet(3);

  const getCellVal = (cell: any): string | number => {
    if (!cell) return "";
    let val = cell.value;
    if (typeof val === "object" && val !== null) {
      if (val instanceof Date) return val.toISOString();
      if ("result" in val) val = val.result;
      else if ("richText" in val)
        val = val.richText.map((t: any) => t.text).join("");
      else if ("text" in val) val = val.text;
      else if ("formula" in val) val = "";
    }
    if (val === null || val === undefined) return "";
    if (typeof val === "string") return val.trim();
    if (typeof val === "number") return val;
    return String(val).trim();
  };

  if (
    !ws1 ||
    String(getCellVal(ws1.getCell("A1"))) !== "ردیف" ||
    String(getCellVal(ws1.getCell("B1"))) !== "نام حساب" ||
    String(getCellVal(ws1.getCell("D1"))) !== "نوع حساب" ||
    String(getCellVal(ws1.getCell("F1"))) !== "رنگ حساب" ||
    String(getCellVal(ws1.getCell("I1"))) !== "نام برچسب" ||
    String(getCellVal(ws1.getCell("K1"))) !== "نوع برچسب" ||
    !ws3 ||
    String(getCellVal(ws3.getCell("A2"))) !== "تاریخ" ||
    String(getCellVal(ws3.getCell("B2"))) !== "ساعت" ||
    String(getCellVal(ws3.getCell("D2"))) !== "نام حساب" ||
    String(getCellVal(ws3.getCell("F2"))) !== "نام برچسب" ||
    String(getCellVal(ws3.getCell("H2"))) !== "نوع تراکنش" ||
    String(getCellVal(ws3.getCell("I2"))) !== "کد دسته‌بندی / مقصد" ||
    String(getCellVal(ws3.getCell("L2"))) !== "مبلغ"
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "اکسل انتخاب‌شده منطبق با نمونه‌ی برنامه نیست، برای ورود داده با اکسل از نمونه‌ی برنامه استفاده کنین",
    );
  }

  const getCellTime = (cell: any): string => {
    if (
      !cell ||
      cell.value === null ||
      cell.value === undefined ||
      cell.value === ""
    )
      return "";
    let val = cell.value;
    if (val instanceof Date) {
      const h = val.getHours();
      const m = val.getMinutes();
      const s = val.getSeconds();
      return `${String(h).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(m).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(s).padStart(2, "0")}`;
    }
    if (val === 0) return ""; // جلوگیری از تبدیل 0 به 00:00:00
    if (typeof val === "number" && val > 0 && val < 1) {
      const totalSeconds = Math.round(val * 24 * 3600);
      const h = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 3600);
      const m = Math.floor((totalSeconds % 3600) / 60);
      const s = totalSeconds % 60;
      return `${String(h).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(m).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(s).padStart(2, "0")}`;
    }
    if (typeof val === "object" && val !== null) {
      if ("result" in val) val = val.result;
      else if ("text" in val) val = val.text;
      else if ("richText" in val)
        val = val.richText.map((t: any) => t.text).join("");
    }
    if (val === 0) return "";
    return String(val || "").trim();
  };

  const getVal = (row: any, cellIdx: number) => {
    let val = getCellVal(row.getCell(cellIdx));
    if (val === "" || val === null || val === undefined) return undefined;
    const parsed = Number(val);
    if (!isNaN(parsed) && val !== "") return parsed;
    return val;
  };

  const uploadedAccounts: any[] = [];
  const uploadedTags: any[] = [];
  const rawTransactions: any[] = [];

  if (ws1) {
    let accOrderFallback = 1000;
    let tagOrderFallback = 1000;

    ws1.eachRow((row, rowNumber) => {
      if (rowNumber === 1) return;

      const orderAccStr = getCellVal(row.getCell(1));
      const nameAcc = getCellVal(row.getCell(2));
      const typeAcc = getCellVal(row.getCell(4));
      const colorAcc = getCellVal(row.getCell(6));

      const orderTagStr = getCellVal(row.getCell(8));
      const nameTag = getCellVal(row.getCell(9));
      const typeTag = getCellVal(row.getCell(11));

      if (nameAcc) {
        if (nameAcc === "وجوه در راه") return;

        const existingAcc = existingAccounts.find(
          (a) => a.name === String(nameAcc),
        );
        const orderAccNum = Number(orderAccStr);
        const finalOrderAcc =
          isNaN(orderAccNum) || orderAccStr === ""
            ? accOrderFallback++
            : orderAccNum;

        if (existingAcc && existingAcc.default) {
          uploadedAccounts.push({
            name: String(nameAcc),
            type: existingAcc.type,
            color: existingAcc.color,
            order: finalOrderAcc,
          });
        } else {
          const typeKey =
            Object.keys(AccountTypes).find(
              (k) => AccountTypes[k as AccountTypesType].name === typeAcc,
            ) || "Bank";
          const colorKey =
            excelColorOptions.find((c) => c.label === colorAcc)?.key || "black";
          uploadedAccounts.push({
            name: String(nameAcc),
            type: typeKey,
            color: colorKey,
            order: finalOrderAcc,
          });
        }
      }
      if (nameTag) {
        const orderTagNum = Number(orderTagStr);
        const finalOrderTag =
          isNaN(orderTagNum) || orderTagStr === ""
            ? tagOrderFallback++
            : orderTagNum;
        const tagTypeKey =
          TagItemTypes.find((t) => t.label === typeTag)?.value || "Other";
        uploadedTags.push({
          name: String(nameTag),
          type: tagTypeKey,
          order: finalOrderTag,
        });
      }
    });
  }

  if (ws3) {
    ws3.eachRow((row, rowNumber) => {
      if (rowNumber <= 2) return;

      const date = getCellVal(row.getCell(1));
      const time = getCellTime(row.getCell(2));
      const accountName = getCellVal(row.getCell(4));
      const tagName = getCellVal(row.getCell(6));

      let typeCode = getCellVal(row.getCell(7));
      if (!typeCode || typeof typeCode !== "string") {
        const typeName = getCellVal(row.getCell(8));
        if (typeName === "دریافت") typeCode = "debit";
        else if (typeName === "پرداخت") typeCode = "credit";
        else if (typeName === "انتقال") typeCode = "transfer";
        else if (typeName === "افتتاح") typeCode = "initial";
        else typeCode = "";
      }

      let catOrDestCode: any = getVal(row, 9);
      if (catOrDestCode === "") catOrDestCode = undefined;

      const note = getCellVal(row.getCell(11));
      const rawValue = getCellVal(row.getCell(12));

      const valueStr = faToEn(String(rawValue || "")).replace(/[^\d]/g, "");
      const value = valueStr ? parseInt(valueStr, 10) : 0;

      const isDateEmpty =
        !date || (typeof date === "string" && date.trim() === "");
      const isAccEmpty =
        !accountName ||
        (typeof accountName === "string" && accountName.trim() === "");

      if (isDateEmpty && isAccEmpty) return;

      rawTransactions.push({
        excelRow: rowNumber,
        date: faToEn(String(date || "")),
        time: faToEn(String(time || "")),
        accountName: String(accountName || ""),
        tagName: String(tagName || ""),
        catOrDestCode,
        type: typeCode,
        note: note ? String(note) : "",
        value,
      });
    });
  }

  const accList: any[] = [];
  const accMap = new Map<string, any>();

  existingAccounts.forEach((a) => {
    const obj = {
      id: a.id,
      name: a.name,
      type: a.type,
      color: a.color,
      order: a.name === "وجوه در راه" ? 999 : a.order,
      default: a.default,
      isNew: false,
      needsUpdate: false,
      txCount: 0,
      finalBalance: 0,
    };
    accList.push(obj);
    accMap.set(String(a.id), obj);
    accMap.set(a.name, obj);
  });

  uploadedAccounts.forEach((a) => {
    if (accMap.has(a.name)) {
      const existing = accMap.get(a.name);
      if (!existing.default) {
        if (existing.type !== a.type || existing.color !== a.color) {
          existing.needsUpdate = true;
          existing.type = a.type;
          existing.color = a.color;
        }
      }
      if (existing.name !== "وجوه در راه") existing.order = a.order;
    } else {
      const obj = {
        ...a,
        default: false,
        isNew: true,
        needsUpdate: false,
        txCount: 0,
        finalBalance: 0,
      };
      accList.push(obj);
      accMap.set(a.name, obj);
    }
  });

  const tagList: any[] = [];
  const tagMap = new Map<string, any>();

  existingTags.forEach((t) => {
    const obj = {
      id: t.id,
      name: t.name,
      type: t.type,
      order: t.order,
      isNew: false,
      needsUpdate: false,
      txCount: 0,
    };
    tagList.push(obj);
    tagMap.set(String(t.id), obj);
    tagMap.set(t.name, obj);
  });

  uploadedTags.forEach((t) => {
    if (tagMap.has(t.name)) {
      const existing = tagMap.get(t.name);
      if (existing.type !== t.type) {
        existing.needsUpdate = true;
        existing.type = t.type;
      }
      existing.order = t.order;
    } else {
      const obj = { ...t, isNew: true, needsUpdate: false, txCount: 0 };
      tagList.push(obj);
      tagMap.set(t.name, obj);
    }
  });

  rawTransactions.forEach((tx) => {
    const accKey = String(tx.accountName);
    if (accMap.has(accKey)) {
      const acc = accMap.get(accKey);
      acc.txCount++;
      if (tx.type === "credit" || tx.type === "transfer")
        acc.finalBalance -= tx.value;
      else acc.finalBalance += tx.value;
    }

    if (tx.type === "transfer") {
      const destKey = String(tx.catOrDestCode);
      if (accMap.has(destKey)) {
        const acc = accMap.get(destKey);
        acc.txCount++;
        acc.finalBalance += tx.value;
      }
    }

    if (tx.tagName && tx.tagName !== "") {
      if (tagMap.has(tx.tagName)) tagMap.get(tx.tagName).txCount++;
    }
  });

  const hasNewData =
    rawTransactions.length > 0 ||
    accList.some((a) => a.isNew) ||
    tagList.some((t) => t.isNew);

  if (!hasNewData) {
    throw new Error(
      "اکسل ارائه‌شده معتبر نیست؛ داده‌های خود را در نمونه‌ی معتبر وارد، و سپس بارگذاری کنید.",
    );
  }

  return {
    accounts: accList.filter((a) => a.txCount > 0 || a.isNew || a.needsUpdate),
    tags: tagList.filter((t) => t.txCount > 0 || t.isNew || t.needsUpdate),
    rawTransactions,
  };
}

===== utility/shared/balancesMethods.ts =====

import { AccountType } from "../accounts/types";
import { TransactionType } from "../transactions/types";
import { AccountBalanceType, BalanceType } from "../balance/types";

/**
 * Calculates the daily balance history for a specific account.
 */
export function getAccountBalanceHistory(
  transactions: TransactionType[],
  accountID: string,
): AccountBalanceType {
  const accTxs = transactions
    .filter((t) => t.debit === accountID || t.credit === accountID)
    .sort((a, b) => a.time - b.time);

  if (accTxs.length === 0) return { id: accountID, history: [] };

  const balances: BalanceType[] = [];
  let balance = 0;

  // پیدا کردن روز اولین تراکنش
  const startDay = new Date(accTxs[0].time);
  startDay.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);

  // روز امروز
  const endDay = new Date();
  endDay.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);

  let currentDay = startDay.getTime();
  let txIndex = 0;

  // حلقه روز به روز تا رسیدن به امروز
  while (currentDay <= endDay.getTime()) {
    // اعمال تمام تراکنش‌هایی که در این روز (currentDay) رخ داده‌اند
    while (
      txIndex < accTxs.length &&
      new Date(accTxs[txIndex].time).setHours(0, 0, 0, 0) === currentDay
    ) {
      const tx = accTxs[txIndex];
      if (tx.debit === accountID) balance += tx.value;
      else if (tx.credit === accountID) balance -= tx.value;
      txIndex++;
    }

    // ثبت موجودی برای این روز
    balances.push({
      time: currentDay,
      balance,
    });

    // رفتن به روز بعد (۲۴ ساعت)
    currentDay += 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  }

  return { id: accountID, history: balances };
}

/**
 * Calculates the daily total balance history across all accounts.
 */
export function getTotalBalanceHistory(
  transactions: TransactionType[],
): BalanceType[] {
  if (!transactions || transactions.length === 0) return [];

  // فیلتر کردن تراکنش‌هایی که روی موجودی کلی تاثیر دارند (transfer تاثیر روی کل ندارد)
  const validTxs = transactions
    .filter((t) => ["initial", "debit", "credit"].includes(t.type))
    .sort((a, b) => a.time - b.time);

  if (validTxs.length === 0) {
    const today = new Date();
    today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    return [{ time: today.getTime(), balance: 0 }];
  }

  const history: BalanceType[] = [];
  let balance = 0;

  const startDay = new Date(validTxs[0].time);
  startDay.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);

  const endDay = new Date();
  endDay.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);

  let currentDay = startDay.getTime();
  let txIndex = 0;

  while (currentDay <= endDay.getTime()) {
    while (
      txIndex < validTxs.length &&
      new Date(validTxs[txIndex].time).setHours(0, 0, 0, 0) === currentDay
    ) {
      const tx = validTxs[txIndex];
      if (tx.type === "initial" || tx.type === "debit") {
        balance += tx.value;
      } else if (tx.type === "credit") {
        balance -= tx.value;
      }
      txIndex++;
    }

    history.push({ time: currentDay, balance });
    currentDay += 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  }

  return history;
}

export function getAccountBalances(
  accounts: AccountType[],
  transactions: TransactionType[],
): AccountBalanceType[] {
  let balances: AccountBalanceType[] = [];
  accounts.forEach((account) => {
    const calculatedbalances = getAccountBalanceHistory(
      transactions.filter(
        (item) => item.debit === account.id || item.credit === account.id,
      ),
      account.id,
    );
    balances.push(calculatedbalances);
  });
  return balances;
}

export function getTotalBalances(balancePoints: AccountBalanceType[]): number {
  let balance = 0;
  balancePoints.forEach(
    (item) => (balance += item.history?.at(-1)?.balance ?? 0),
  );
  return balance;
}

===== utility/shared/formatters.ts =====

import {
  jalaliToTimestamp,
  toEnglishDigits,
  toJalaliDateArray,
} from "@/utility/shared/timeMethods";

export const toPersianDigits = (value: string) =>
  value.replace(/\d/g, (d) => Number(d).toLocaleString("fa-IR"));

function isJalaliLeapYear(year: number): boolean {
  const r = year % 33;
  return (
    r === 1 ||
    r === 5 ||
    r === 9 ||
    r === 13 ||
    r === 17 ||
    r === 22 ||
    r === 26 ||
    r === 30
  );
}

export function formatJalaliDate(timestamp: number): string {
  if (!timestamp) return "";
  const [year, month, day] = toJalaliDateArray(new Date(timestamp));
  return toPersianDigits(
    `${year}/${month.toString().padStart(2, "0")}/${day.toString().padStart(2, "0")}`,
  );
}

export function formatTimeInput(timestamp: number): string {
  if (!timestamp) return "";
  const date = new Date(timestamp);
  const hours = date.getHours().toString().padStart(2, "0");
  const minutes = date.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, "0");
  return toPersianDigits(`${hours}:${minutes}`);
}

export function parseJalaliInput(formattedValue: string): number {
  const raw = toEnglishDigits(formattedValue).replace(/\D/g, "");
  if (raw.length !== 8) return 0;
  const year = parseInt(raw.slice(0, 4), 10);
  const month = parseInt(raw.slice(4, 6), 10);
  const day = parseInt(raw.slice(6, 8), 10);
  if (year >= 1300 && month >= 1 && month <= 12 && day >= 1 && day <= 31) {
    return jalaliToTimestamp([year, month, day]);
  }
  return 0;
}

export function parseTimeInput(formattedValue: string): number {
  const raw = toEnglishDigits(formattedValue).replace(/\D/g, "");
  if (raw.length !== 4) return 0;
  const hours = parseInt(raw.slice(0, 2), 10);
  const minutes = parseInt(raw.slice(2, 4), 10);
  if (hours < 0 || hours > 23 || minutes < 0 || minutes > 59) return 0;
  const now = new Date();
  now.setHours(hours, minutes, 0, 0);
  return now.getTime();
}

export function mergeDateTime(
  baseTimestamp: number,
  newTimestamp: number,
  part: "date" | "time",
): number {
  const base = new Date(baseTimestamp);
  const incoming = new Date(newTimestamp);
  const result = new Date(base);

  if (part === "date") {
    result.setFullYear(
      incoming.getFullYear(),
      incoming.getMonth(),
      incoming.getDate(),
    );
  } else {
    result.setHours(
      incoming.getHours(),
      incoming.getMinutes(),
      incoming.getSeconds(),
      0,
    );
  }
  return result.getTime();
}

// ==================== فرمت‌بندی هوشمند تاریخ ====================
export function formatJalaliInput(
  value: string,
  prevValue: string = "",
): string {
  const persianValue = toEnglishDigits(value).replace(/[^\d/]/g, "");
  const prevPersianValue = toEnglishDigits(prevValue).replace(/[^\d/]/g, "");
  const isDeleting = persianValue.length < prevPersianValue.length;

  let parts = persianValue.split("/");
  let yearStr = parts[0]?.slice(0, 4) || "";
  let monthStr = parts[1]?.slice(0, 2) || "";
  let dayStr = parts[2]?.slice(0, 2) || "";

  if (parts.length === 1) {
    const d = parts[0].slice(0, 8);
    if (d.length <= 4) yearStr = d;
    else if (d.length === 5) {
      yearStr = d.slice(0, 4);
      monthStr = d[4];
    } else if (d.length === 6) {
      yearStr = d.slice(0, 4);
      monthStr = d.slice(4, 6);
    } else if (d.length === 7) {
      yearStr = d.slice(0, 4);
      monthStr = d.slice(4, 6);
      dayStr = d[6];
    } else if (d.length === 8) {
      yearStr = d.slice(0, 4);
      monthStr = d.slice(4, 6);
      dayStr = d.slice(6, 8);
    }
  }

  if (yearStr.length > 0 && yearStr.length < 3) yearStr = "140";
  if (yearStr.length === 4 && parseInt(yearStr) < 1404) yearStr = "1404";

  let res = yearStr;
  let monthNum = 0;

  const showFirstSlash =
    persianValue.includes("/") ||
    monthStr.length > 0 ||
    (yearStr.length === 4 && !isDeleting);

  if (showFirstSlash) {
    res += "/";

    if (monthStr.length > 0) {
      monthNum = parseInt(monthStr) || 0;
      if (monthStr.length === 1) {
        if (
          (monthStr === "0" || monthStr === "1" || monthStr === "2") &&
          !persianValue.endsWith("/")
        ) {
          res += monthStr;
          return toPersianDigits(res);
        } else {
          res += monthStr.padStart(2, "0");
          monthStr = monthStr.padStart(2, "0");
        }
      } else {
        monthNum = Math.min(12, monthNum);
        res += monthNum.toString().padStart(2, "0");
        monthStr = monthNum.toString().padStart(2, "0");
      }
    }

    const monthComplete = monthStr.length === 2;
    const showSecondSlash =
      persianValue.includes("/", persianValue.indexOf("/") + 1) ||
      dayStr.length > 0 ||
      (monthComplete && !isDeleting);

    if (showSecondSlash) {
      res += "/";

      if (dayStr.length > 0) {
        const isLeap = isJalaliLeapYear(parseInt(yearStr) || 0);
        let maxDay = 31;
        if (monthNum > 6 && monthNum < 12) maxDay = 30;
        if (monthNum === 12) maxDay = isLeap ? 30 : 29;

        let dayNum = parseInt(dayStr) || 0;
        if (dayStr.length === 1) {
          if (dayStr === "0") {
            res += "0"; 
            return toPersianDigits(res);
          } else if (
            (dayStr === "1" || dayStr === "2" || dayStr === "3") &&
            !persianValue.endsWith("/")
          ) {
            if (monthNum === 12 && !isLeap && dayStr === "3") {
              res += "03";
            } else {
              res += dayStr;
              return toPersianDigits(res);
            }
          } else {
            res += dayStr.padStart(2, "0");
          }
        } else {
          dayNum = Math.min(maxDay, dayNum);
          res += dayNum.toString().padStart(2, "0");
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return toPersianDigits(res);
}

// ==================== فرمت‌بندی هوشمند ساعت ====================
export function formatTimeTyping(
  value: string,
  prevValue: string = "",
): string {
  const persianValue = toEnglishDigits(value).replace(/[^\d:]/g, "");
  const prevPersianValue = toEnglishDigits(prevValue).replace(/[^\d:]/g, "");
  const isDeleting = persianValue.length < prevPersianValue.length;

  let parts = persianValue.split(":");
  let hourStr = parts[0]?.slice(0, 2) || "";
  let minStr = parts[1]?.slice(0, 2) || "";

  if (parts.length === 1) {
    const d = parts[0].slice(0, 4);
    if (d.length <= 2) hourStr = d;
    else if (d.length === 3) {
      hourStr = d.slice(0, 2);
      minStr = d[2];
    } else if (d.length === 4) {
      hourStr = d.slice(0, 2);
      minStr = d.slice(2, 4);
    }
  }

  let res = hourStr;

  if (hourStr.length === 2) {
    let h = Math.min(23, parseInt(hourStr) || 0);
    res = h.toString().padStart(2, "0");
    hourStr = res;
  } else if (hourStr.length === 1) {
    if (parseInt(hourStr) >= 3) {
      res = hourStr.padStart(2, "0");
      hourStr = res;
    } else {
      res = hourStr;
      return toPersianDigits(res);
    }
  }

  const hourComplete = hourStr.length === 2;
  const showColon =
    persianValue.includes(":") ||
    minStr.length > 0 ||
    (hourComplete && !isDeleting);

  if (showColon) {
    res += ":";

    if (minStr.length > 0) {
      if (minStr.length === 1) {
        if (parseInt(minStr) >= 6) {
          res += minStr.padStart(2, "0");
        } else {
          res += minStr;
        }
      } else {
        let m = Math.min(59, parseInt(minStr) || 0);
        res += m.toString().padStart(2, "0");
      }
    }
  }

  return toPersianDigits(res);
}

===== utility/shared/idGenerationMethods.ts =====

import { AccountType } from "@/utility/accounts/types";
import { TagType } from "./tagTypes";

export const generateNextAccountId = (
  existingAccounts: AccountType[],
): number => {
  if (existingAccounts.length === 0) {
    return 0x01020002;
  }

  const maxId = Math.max(...existingAccounts.map((acc) => Number(acc.id)));

  if (maxId >= 0x01020000 && maxId < 0x01020010) {
    return maxId + 1;
  }

  throw new Error("تعداد حساب‌ها از حد مجاز بیشتر شده است");
};

export const generateNextTagId = (existingTags: TagType[]): number => {
  if (existingTags.length === 0) {
    return 0x01030000;
  }

  const maxId = Math.max(...existingTags.map((acc) => Number(acc.id)));

  return maxId + 1;
};

export const generateUUId = () => {
  if (typeof crypto !== "undefined" && typeof crypto.randomUUID === "function")
    return crypto.randomUUID();
  return "xxxxxxxx-xxxx-4xxx-yxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx".replace(/[xy]/g, (c) => {
    const r = (Math.random() * 16) | 0;
    const v = c == "x" ? r : (r & 0x3) | 0x8;
    return v.toString(16);
  });
};

===== utility/shared/timeMethods.ts =====

import { toGregorian } from "jalaali-js";

export function toEnglishDigits(value: string): string {
  return value
    .replace(/[۰-۹]/g, (d) => "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹".indexOf(d).toString())
    .replace(/[٠-٩]/g, (d) => "٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩".indexOf(d).toString());
}

export function toJalaliDateArray(date: Date): number[] {
  return date
    .toLocaleDateString("fa-ir", {
      year: "numeric",
      month: "numeric",
      day: "numeric",
    })
    .split("/")
    .map((item) => Number(toEnglishDigits(item)));
}

export function pastDaysInYear(date: number[]): number {
  const [, month, day] = date;

  let days = day;

  if (month <= 6) days += (month - 1) * 31;
  if (month > 6 && month < 12) days += 186 + (month - 7) * 30;
  if (month === 12) days += 336;

  return days;
}

export function jalaliToTimestamp([year, month, day]: [
  number,
  number,
  number,
]): number {
  const { gy, gm, gd } = toGregorian(year, month, day);

  return new Date(gy, gm - 1, gd).getTime();
}

===== utility/shared/transactionsMethods.ts =====

import { TransactionCategories } from "./transactionCategories";
import { TransactionType } from "../transactions/types";

/**
 * Returns the most recent transactions.
 *
 * Transactions are sorted in descending order by their timestamp, ensuring
 * that the newest transactions appear first. The original array is not modified.
 *
 * @param transactions Collection of transactions to process.
 * @param limit Maximum number of transactions to return.
 *
 * @returns An array containing up to the specified number of most recent transactions.
 */
export function getLatestTransactions(
  transactions: TransactionType[],
  limit: number,
): TransactionType[] {
  return [...transactions].sort((a, b) => b.time - a.time).slice(0, limit);
}

export function getCategoryById(id: string) {
  for (const cat of TransactionCategories.expense) {
    if (cat.id === Number(id)) return cat;
    if (cat.sub) {
      const found = cat.sub.find((sub) => sub.id === Number(id));
      if (found) return found;
    }
  }

  for (const cat of TransactionCategories.income) {
    if (cat.id === Number(id)) return cat;
    if (cat.sub) {
      const found = cat.sub.find((sub) => sub.id === Number(id));
      if (found) return found;
    }
  }

  return null;
}

===== utility/shared/types.ts =====

import { TransactionCategories } from "./transactionCategories";

/**
 * Top-level transaction category.
 *
 * Derived from the keys of {@link TransactionCategories}.
 */

export type TransactionCategoryGroupType = keyof typeof TransactionCategories;
/**
 * Transaction category within a top-level category.
 *
 * Derived from {@link TransactionCategories}.
 */

export type TransactionCategoryType = keyof (typeof TransactionCategories)[TransactionCategoryGroupType][number];
/**
 * Transaction subcategory.
 *
 * Derived from the available expense subcategories in
 * {@link TransactionCategories}.
 */

export type TransactionSubCategoryType = keyof (typeof TransactionCategories.expense)[number]["sub"][number];

===== utility/shared/usePageShowSync.ts =====

import { useEffect } from "react";

export function usePageShowSync(callback: () => void, deps: any[] = []) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleVisibilityChange = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === "visible") callback();
    };

    window.addEventListener("focus", callback);
    window.addEventListener("pageshow", callback);
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", handleVisibilityChange);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("focus", callback);
      window.removeEventListener("pageshow", callback);
      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", handleVisibilityChange);
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, deps);
}

===== utility/transactions/types.ts =====

/**
 * Represents the kind of financial transaction.
 *
 * - `debit`    : Money received by an account.
 * - `credit`   : Money paid from an account.
 * - `transfer` : Money transferred between two accounts.
 * - `initial`  : Initial account balance.
 */
export type TransactionKindType = "debit" | "credit" | "transfer" | "initial";

/**
 * Represents a financial transaction.
 */
export type TransactionType = {
  /** Unique transaction identifier. */
  id: string;

  /** Unix timestamp representing when the transaction occurred. */
  time: number;

  /** User-defined transaction title or description. */
  name: string;

  /** Identifier of the debit (destination) account. */
  debit: string;

  /** Identifier of the credit (source) account. */
  credit: string;

  /** Transaction amount. */
  value: number;

  /** Transaction kind. */
  type: TransactionKindType;

  tag?: string;

  note?: string;
};
